# BRACU CSE Faculty Profiles

## Md Faisal Ahmed

- **Source URL**: https://cse.bracu.ac.bd/faculty_profile/16/md_faisal_ahmed

### Profile Details

- **Name**: Md Faisal Ahmed
- **Designation**: Lecturer
- **Email**: faisal.ahmed@bracu.ac.bd
- **Websites**: Available on portal

### Biography

Md Faisal Ahmed is a graduate of Computer Science, achieving Vice-Chancellor's Gold Medal in the 15th Convocation of Brac University. He joined Brac University as a faculty member in 2022 and previously served as a security researcher at Beetles Cyber Security Limited. He amassed a handful of security 'hall of fame' acknowledgments from several renowned multinational companies such as Adobe, Hackerrank, Imgur, Sony, Tesla, Zendesk, etc.

His research and development areas span the fields of Machine learning in Cyber Security, Malware Analysis, Natural Language Processing, and Social Media Analysis.

He used to be a Minecraft veteran and enjoys watching anime. Additionally, he has a penchant for collecting items. Currently, he possesses a black sword ([**Yubashiri**](https://onepiece.fandom.com/wiki/Yubashiri)) and has intentions to expand his collection with more.

**Ahmed, M. F.,** Mahmud, Z., Biash, Z. T., Ryen, A. A. N., Hossain, A., & Ashraf, F. B. (2021). Bangla text dataset and exploratory analysis for online harassment detection. *arXiv preprint arXiv:2102.02478*.

## Gazi Hasin Ishrak

- **Source URL**: https://cse.bracu.ac.bd/faculty_profile/17/gazi_hasin_ishrak

### Profile Details

- **Name**: Gazi Hasin Ishrak
- **Designation**: Adjunct Lecturer
- **Email**: ext.gazi.hasin@bracu.ac.bd

### Biography

Gazi Hasin Ishrak is a highly skilled Contractual Lecturer at the Computer Science and Engineering Department of Brac University. With a strong background in the field, his expertise lies in teaching system analysis design, Artificial Intelligence, computer networks, computer graphics.

##### Background

Ishrak’s journey began with a Bachelor's degree in Computer Science from Brac University. He quickly developed a keen interest in software development and excelled in several courses relevant to his pursued program, including – Ai, Data Structures, and Database Systems. He has been working remotely as a Software Developer at a reputed company situated in the UK for the past three and a half years. His work primarily revolves around developing and managing software systems, implementing Ai algorithms, optimizing data management, and ensuring data security.  The company offered him a position with very flexible hours so it did not disrupt his studies, while also developing related skills during his undergraduate program.

As a Contractual Lecturer, Ishrak excels in sharing his knowledge and expertise with students. He has a remarkable ability to break down complex concepts and explain them in a way that students find engaging and easy to understand. his teaching style encourages active learning and fosters a deep understanding of the subjects he teaches.

Ishrak’s passion for software and data management is evident in his dedication to staying up-to-date with the latest industry trends and technologies. He actively seeks opportunities to expand his knowledge and refine his skills, ensuring that he provides the most relevant and cutting-edge information to his students.

## Dr. Md Sadek Ferdous

- **Source URL**: https://cse.bracu.ac.bd/faculty_profile/22/dr_md_sadek_ferdous

### Profile Details

- **Name**: Dr. Md Sadek Ferdous

### Research Interest

My current research interests lie in the intersection of Self-sovereign Identity (SSI), Blockchain, Trust, Security and Privacy.

### Biography

Dr. Ferdous is a Professor at the Department of Computer Science and Engineering of the BRAC University, Dhaka, Bangladesh. He is also an Honorary Research Fellow at the Institute for Security Science and Technology of Imperial College London.

Previously, he was an ERCIM (European Research Consortium for Informatics and Mathematics) Alain Bensoussan Research Fellow at Fraunhofer Institute for Applied Information Technology (FIT), Sankt Augustin, Germany. During this one-year research fellowship he researched on Self-sovereign Identity Management using Blockchain Technology. He was supervised by Prof. Wolfgang Prinz.

Previously, he was an Assistant Professor at the Department of Computer Science and Engineering of the Shahjalal University of Science and Technology (SUST), Sylhet Bangladesh. During my SUST tenure, I established the first-ever blockchain-focused security research laboratory, called SUBSEL (Shahjalal University Blockchain and SEcurity Laboratory), in Bangladesh at SUST. Before joining SUST I worked as a Research Associate at the Centre for Financial Technology of the Imperial College Business School, Imperial College London, UK. I was involved in a project in collaboration with Citi Group to explore how the blockchain technology could be leveraged to improve email security for any global financial institution. I am still associated with the Centre to continue this research remotely from Bangladesh.

While at Imperial, Dr. Ferdous had the opportunity to work at the UK Cabinet Office as their first Technology Fellow. He was a part of a joint-project involving the UK Cabinet Office, UK Ministry of Justice and UK Police in which I explored how the blockchain technology could be leveraged for securing digital evidences. He designed and developed a proof of concept which the UK ministry of Justice exploring the possibility for real-life trial. This has been a very successful project and it received world-wide media attention. Please check the Media link to view a few media coverages. He worked as a Research Fellow at the Electronics and Computer Science of the University of Southampton in a Horizon 2020 cloud security project called SUNFISH in between September, 2015 to June, 2017. In this project, it was investigated how the blockchain technology could be exploited to increase the security in a cloud federation. He was a Post-doctoral Research Assistant at the School of Computing Science of the University of Glasgow in a project called the Integrated Multimedia City Data (iMCD). This was part of an ESRC funded Urban Big Data Centre, Glasgow under the umbrella of Big Data Theme. He was responsible for conducting research and developing systems for collating a large collection of textual and multimedia data from online news sources, microblogs, social sharing sites such as Youtube & flickr and wearable sensors such as life-loggers and GPS devices and then to analyse this data set to make sense.

Dr. Ferdous was a SICSA (Scottish Informatics and Computer Science Alliance) Funded, PhD student at the University of Glasgow. My thesis title is User-controlled Identity Management Systems using Mobile Devices in which I investigated how modern smartphones, ever increasing in power and size, could be used for managing our online identities. My primary supervisor was Dr. Ron Poet and secondary supervisor was Dr. Gethin Norman.

Dr. Ferdous actively advise the Government of Bangladesh and different other Bangladeshi organisations in different capacities, particularly in Blockchain. He is the principal author of the National Blockchain Strategy document of Bangladesh.

## Ms. Khondoker Nazia Iqbal

### Research Interest

Machine Learning

AI

Medical Image Processing, Mental Health

### Biography

Khondoker Nazia Iqbal is currently working as a Lecturer in the Computer Science and Engineering Department of BRAC University. She completed her Bachelor of Science in CSE at BRAC University with the Highest Distinction and is currently pursuing her MSc degree at the same university. During her undergraduate years, as a recognition of academic excellence, she was awarded the Vice Chancellor’s Honor List nine times and the Dean’s Honor List twice. She was also awarded a Merit Scholarship in recognition of her outstanding academic performance in both BSc and MSc. 

As a recognition of her hard work and innovative teaching methods, she received the Excellence in Teaching Award for demonstrating innovation and excellence in teaching. Beyond teaching, she plays a vital role in course coordination to ensure smooth academic operations and curriculum management. Her dedication to academic management has been recognized with a certification in course coordination. She actively collaborates with faculty members to enhance course structures, streamline assessments, and improve the overall learning experience for students.

Nazia’s passion for research lies in exploring advancements in Machine learning, Computer Vision, and Medical Image Processing. Through her publications in national and international conferences, she actively contributes to the research community. With a strong background in teaching and research, Khondoker Nazia Iqbal is dedicated to academic excellence, mentoring students, and expanding knowledge in computer science.

## Dr. Muhammad Iqbal Hossain

- **Source URL**: https://cse.bracu.ac.bd/faculty_profile/24/dr_muhammad_iqbal_hossain

### Profile Details

- **Name**: Dr. Muhammad Iqbal Hossain

### Research Interest

Information security (Cryptography, Access control, Cloud Security, IoT security, etc)  
Artificial Intelligence and Machine learning  
Model-based Software Engineering, Software Testing and Verification., Automation, etc.

**Slot booked till Summer 2027 (Undergraduate).**

### Biography

Dr. Muhammad Iqbal Hossain is an Associate Professor of the Computer Science and Engineering Department of BRAC University. Significant achievements and rigorous pursuits mark his academic journey. Dr. Hossain earned his Ph.D. in Computer Engineering from Kyungpook National University, South Korea, in January 2018. During his doctoral tenure, he actively participated in the Embedded Software Engineering Lab. His contributions to this lab resulted in a substantial collection of peer-reviewed journals and presentations at both international and domestic conferences in Korea. Dr. Hossain's commitment to staying at the forefront of his field is evident through his active participation in various international conferences and symposiums, which have enriched his perspectives and insights.

Dr. Hossain's scholarly pursuits have garnered him recognition beyond his academic accomplishments. His expertise has led him to assume significant roles as a Reviewer and Editorial Board Member for reputable international journals and conferences. Moreover, his remarkable academic journey was facilitated by prestigious scholarships, including the Korean Government Scholarship (GKS) in 2009, which enabled his graduate studies. Subsequently, his exceptional dedication and contributions were acknowledged with the KNU Honors Scholarship during his doctoral pursuit.

Dr. Iqbal’s research interests primarily focus on Information security (Including Cryptography, Access control, and privacy), AI and Machine learning, Model-based Software Engineering, Software Testing and Verification, and automation.

## Ms. Tasnim Ferdous

- **Source URL**: https://cse.bracu.ac.bd/faculty_profile/26/ms_tasnim_ferdous

### Profile Details

- **Name**: Ms. Tasnim Ferdous
- **Designation**: Senior Lecturer
- **Email**: tasnim.ferdous@bracu.ac.bd

### Office Location / Address

- CSE Department
- 4th floor, Room No # 4K73,
- Brac University,
- Kha 224 Bir Uttam Rafiqul Islam Avenue,
- Merul Badda, Dhaka, Bangladesh

### Research Interest

My research focuses on **learning, memory, and personalized cognitive training**. I study how people learn and remember, and how technology can support memory improvement, cognitive performance, and sleep quality.

My work combines **cognitive neuroscience, artificial intelligence, neuroadaptive systems, and brain-computer interface ideas**. I am especially interested in developing AI-based and interactive systems for memory training, sleep analysis, and human-centered cognitive technologies.

**Research interests:**

1. 1. Brain–Computer Interfaces and Neuroadaptive Systems
2. 2. Cognitive Neuroscience, Learning, Memory, and Cognitive Training
3. 3. Eye-Tracking and Attention Analysis
4. 4. Sleep, Cognition, and Intelligent Health Technologies
5. 5. Artificial Intelligence and Deep Learning
6. 6. Gesture-Based Human–Computer Interaction and Applied Computer Vision

### Biography

Tasnim Ferdous is a Senior Lecturer in the Department of Computer Science and Engineering at BRAC University. She earned her M.Eng. in Electrical and Computer Engineering from Oregon State University (2016), where she received the Laurel Award, and her B.Sc. in Electrical and Electronic Engineering from American International University-Bangladesh (AIUB) in 2012.

Her research in computational cognitive neuroscience focuses on learning and memory across wakefulness and sleep. She combines adaptive cognitive training, physiological signal analysis, and interpretable AI. Her interests include personalized working-memory training, the Method of Loci, mental imagery, and VR-based route navigation.

Her sleep research builds on published work in EEG- and PPG-based sleep-stage classification and explores how AI can help interpret and predict brain activity during sleep. Targeted memory reactivation (TMR) is an exploratory interest for future research, investigating whether cues during sleep, linked to earlier learning, can support memory consolidation and later recall.

She supervises undergraduate research in sleep analysis, assistive human–computer interaction (HCI), and applied computer vision. Previously, she taught at Northern University Bangladesh and completed an electrical engineering internship at Electro Scientific Industries (ESI) in the United States.

Outside academia, she enjoys sketching, reading across a variety of topics, hiking, and experimenting with new recipes.

[Google Scholar](https://scholar.google.com/citations?user=9zwLZtMAAAAJ&hl=en) | [ORCID](https://orcid.org/0009-0001-6469-8319)

### Research Interest

My research focuses on **learning, memory, and personalized cognitive training**. I study how people learn and remember, and how adaptive technologies can support memory improvement.

My work combines **cognitive neuroscience, neuroadaptive systems, and AI**. I am especially interested in memory training, mnemonic strategies, and sleep-related brain activity that may support learning and cognitive recovery. I work on these areas:  
  
1. Cognitive Neuroscience,   
2. Memory Enhancement,   
3. Brain-Computer Interfaces,   
4. Artificial Intelligence,   
5. Sleep Quality  
  
**Open Positions**

I am looking for **motivated undergraduate or master’s students** to join my research on **the Method of Loci, VR-inspired educational game design in Unity, Sustained attention through eye-tracking, and Computer Vision**.

### How to Apply

Send your **CV + project interest** to: tasnim.ferdous@bracu.ac.bd

### Research Projects

Research Overview
-----------------

Computational cognitive neuroscience researcher studying how human memory is **encoded, maintained, consolidated, and retrieved across wakefulness and sleep**. My work combines adaptive cognitive training, interpretable EEG analysis, and computational modeling to connect wakeful learning with sleep-dependent memory consolidation.

**Memory During Wakefulness**  
I investigate how adaptive working-memory training and mnemonic strategies, including the Method of Loci, can support learning and recall. My current interests include VR-enhanced memory environments, mental imagery, and item–locus binding.

**Memory During Sleep**  
I explore how AI and EEG can help us understand and predict brain activity during sleep. Targeted memory reactivation (TMR) as a future research direction: whether carefully timed cues linked to earlier learning can strengthen memories during sleep and improve later recall.

My research focuses on understanding and enhancing memory through adaptive technologies informed by behavior and brain physiology. Alongside this work, I supervise *undergraduate research in sleep analysis, assistive human–computer interaction (HCI), and applied computer vision.*

Student Research Opportunities

I welcome inquiries from motivated undergraduate and master’s students interested in memory training and the Method of Loci, VR-based learning environments, EEG sleep analysis, assistive interaction, or applied computer vision. Projects involve experimental design, data collection and analysis, computational modeling, or interactive system development.

To express interest, email ([tasnim.ferdous@bracu.ac.bd](mailto:tasnim.ferdous@bracu.ac.bd)) your **CV and a short description of the project or research area you would like to explore**.

Research Projects and Supervision
---------------------------------

I mentor undergraduate researchers in study design, model development, experimental evaluation, result analysis, and manuscript preparation. The projects below include supervised research and my own work on adaptive interaction.

### Sleep Research

#### 1. Transfer Learning for EEG Sleep Staging | *Ongoing supervised research*

This project investigates how EEG sleep-stage classifiers generalize across datasets. We are examining transfer learning approaches to improve classification under dataset shift, with an emphasis on Macro-F1 performance and adaptation to new data with limited training.

**Team:** Tasnim Ferdous and Naveed Mahmood.

#### 2. Explainable EEG Sleep Stage Classification | *Completed and published at ICITEE, Bangkok, Thailand, 2025*

We developed **GradG-AttnSleep**, an interpretable sleep-staging framework built on an attention-based CNN. The work applies dual-mode Grad-CAM to examine the EEG patterns contributing to model decisions and support physiologically oriented interpretation of predicted sleep stages.

**Team:** Tasnim Ferdous, Naveed Mahmood, and Aniqua Nusrat Zereen.    
  
3. Explainable PPG Sleep Stage Classification | *Completed with two IEEE conference publications in 2024*

This work investigated machine learning and explainable AI for sleep-stage classification from photoplethysmography (PPG). I served as first author on two papers and co-supervised undergraduate teams examining classification approaches and informative physiological features.

**Team:** Tasnim Ferdous, Aniqua Nusrat Zereen, Reshad Ul Karim, Abrar Samin, Sammam Mahdi, and Himika Tasnim.   
  
**Publications:**  
    • N. S. Mahmud, **T. Ferdous**, and A. N. Zereen. “[GradG-AttnSleep: Dual-Mode GradCAM for Interpretable Sleep Staging with an Attention-Based CNN](https://ieeexplore.ieee.org/abstract/document/11338388/).” *Proceedings of the 17th International Conference on   Information Technology and Electrical Engineering (ICITEE 2025), Bangkok, Thailand, 2025.*

• **T. Ferdous**, R. U. Karim, A. Samin, S. Mahdi, and A. N. Zereen.[“Improved Photoplethysmography-Based Four-Stage Sleep Classification with Explainable AI-Driven Machine Learning,”](https://ieeexplore.ieee.org/document/10898853) *2024 IEEE 2nd International Conference on Electrical, Automation and Computer Engineering (ICEACE), Changchun, China, 2024, pp. 117-122.*

• **T. Ferdous**, R. U. Karim, A. Samin, S. Mahdi, H. Tasnim, and A. N. Zereen.[“Machine Learning Approaches in Photoplethysmography-Based Sleep Stage Classification,”](https://ieeexplore.ieee.org/document/10898858) *2024 IEEE 2nd International Conference on Electrical, Automation and Computer Engineering (ICEACE), Changchun, China, 2024, pp. 123-128.*

### Assistive HCI and Applied Computer Vision

#### 1. Behavioral Closed Loop Adaptation for Working Memory | *Research led by Tasnim Ferdous; study completed in 2026*

This project asks whether gameplay behavior can guide personalized difficulty regulation in active-recall working-memory tasks. Developed a sensor-free controller that uses accuracy, relative reaction time, omissions, repeated errors, and fatigue-related slowing to adjust task difficulty. A five-day study with 81 participants evaluated its ability to provide individualized, behavior-responsive adaptation.

#### 2. GestureBraille for Accessible Text Entry | *Ongoing supervised project; 2026*

Conceptualized and supervising GestureBraille, a computer vision-based assistive input system designed to support symbolic text entry for visually impaired users. The system translates hand gestures into Braille-cell six-dot selections.

**Team:** Tasnim Ferdous, Mostakim Mahmud Mugdhha, and Farida Chowdhury.

#### 3. Interpretable Drowsiness Detection | Upcoming

This project develops a real-time drowsiness detection system for classroom and driver-monitoring settings. It combines MediaPipe-based region extraction, deep learning, Grad-CAM interpretation, and temporal smoothing. Evaluation examines performance under varied lighting and motion conditions.

DOI: <https://doi.org/10.1007/s42979-022-01367-8>

***Conference*****:**

**Pathfinder: A Fog Assisted Vision-Based System for Optimal Path Selection of Service Robots**

Niloy Irtisam, **Riad Ahmed**, Mohammad Moniruzzaman Akash, Raiyaan Abdullah, Sujan Sarker, Sejuti Rahman, Lafifa Jamal

Publication date : 2020/8/26

Conference : 2020 Joint 9th International Conference on Informatics, Electronics & Vision (ICIEV) and 2020 4th International Conference on Imaging, Vision & Pattern Recognition (icIVPR)

Publisher : IEEE

DOI: <https://doi.org/10.1109/ICIEVicIVPR48672.2020.9306573>

### Education

* University of Dhaka (B.Sc. in Robotics and Mechatronics Engineering)
* Agricultural University College, Mymensingh (HSC)
* Ichail High School,Mymensingh (SSC)

### Research Experiences

**1.  Research Assistant - Supervisor: Prof. Dr. Lafifa Jamal**  
**(University of Dhaka)**  
***Haate-khori: An AI-based Bengali Handwriting Teaching Robot for Children:*** A robot whose purpose is to teach children handwriting in an interactive manner. A vision-  
based system identifies and evaluates the quality of handwriting using two separate deep  
neural network systems- one for letter detection and another(consisting of 11 separate net-  
works) for quality detection.

**2 .  Research Assistant - Supervisor: Dr. Sejuti Rahman**  
**(University of Dhaka)**  
***IHABOT: Intelligent Hospital Assistance Robot to Fight Contagion by Reducing Doctor-Patient Interaction :***

The proposed Intelligent Hospital Assistance Robot (IHABOT) is equipped with a number of autonomous features. It can  
map its surroundings, determine the best route to take to its destination, and hence navigate by itself in a real-world  
environment. It has a variety of sensors to collect physiological data from the patient. IHABOT uses artificial intelligence  
(AI) to automatically evaluate these physiological measures and detect patients who are deteriorating early.

**Md. Tawhid Anwar** is currently working as a Senior Lecturer in the Computer Science and Engineering department of BRAC University. Prior to this, he served as an Undergraduate Teaching Assistant (aka Student Tutor) in the Department of CSE for almost 2 years. In addition, he was also a Student Mentor, where his responsibility was to guide fresher students in their academic improvement. Tawhid completed his M.Sc. in Computer Science and Engineering from BRAC University with an outstanding academic record. He is a dedicated, hardworking, and decisive individual. He completed his B.Sc. in Computer Science and Engineering from BRAC University.

Tawhid oversees the BRAC University Entrepreneurship Development Forum - BUEDF club as the faculty co-advisor. He is well known for his leadership skills in managing a team noted for its high level of collaboration. He strives to contribute to the greater benefit of students through knowledge and wisdom. His research interests include Artificial Intelligence, Financial Market Analysis, and Machine Learning.

### Research Interest

I have a keen interest in ***cyber security*** and ***blockchain*** technologies. I find the idea of securing digital systems and protecting sensitive data fascinating. Additionally, the concept of blockchain and its potential to revolutionize various industries captivates me. Moreover, I am particularly intrigued by the field of ***Threat Intelligence***, as it involves gathering and analyzing information to proactively identify and mitigate cyber security risks.

I am actively exploring the domain of **image processing and computer vision**, particularly in the context of **security and threat detection**. I am interested in how visual data can be analyzed using machine learning and deep learning techniques to support applications such as surveillance analytics, anomaly detection, biometric systems, and digital image forensics.

In addition to my interests in cybersecurity and blockchain technologies, I am also deeply interested in ***software engineering***. I am motivated to create software systems that are secure, reliable and meet the highest standards of quality and performance.

- **Student Level**: Undergraduate

### Research Interest

**Software Engineering**

* Large Language Model (LLM) Based Software Engineering Development
* Qualitative Analysis of AI-assisted Code Generation Tools
* Empirical Software Engineering
* Automated Code Analysis and Error Detection Tools

### Biography

MD. Fakhruddin Gazzali is a respected lecturer in Computer Science and Engineering Dept. at BRAC University. He earned his Bachelor of Science degree (BSc.) in the same field from the Islamic University of Technology (IUT), demonstrating his commitment to his discipline from the outset of his career.

At BRAC University, Mr. Gazzali is currently teaching courses in Algorithms, Data Communication, and Object Oriented Programming. These subjects are fundamental to the field of Computer Science and Engineering, and his expertise in these areas greatly benefits his students. Before his current role at BRAC, Mr. Gazzali served as a lecturer at United International University (UIU), where he taught a range of these subjects including Structured Programming Language. His teaching prowess was particularly evident in his DBMS and Algorithm course, where all the teams he guided became champions or achieved other top honors, reflecting his effective mentorship and the quality of his instruction.

In addition to his academic roles, Mr. Gazzali has also gained practical industry experience as a Software Developer Intern at Nilavo Technologies. This experience has enriched his teaching and research, providing him with valuable insights into the practical applications of computer science. Beyond his professional responsibilities, Mr. Gazzali is an active volunteer with the Mozilla Bangladesh Organization and the Firefox Social Support Team, demonstrating his commitment to the broader tech community.

Mr. Gazzali's research interests span Software Engineering, Computer Vision, and Computer Networks. His work in these areas is driven by his passion for exploring new frontiers in technology. Further attesting to his expertise, Mr. Gazzali holds a CCNA certificate, a globally recognized IT certification. His diverse interests and accomplishments reflect his dedication to continuous learning and contribution to the field of computer science.

### Biography

Tasnim Ahsan Prome completed her Bachelor's degree in Computer Science and Engineering from BRAC University with highest distinction. She completed her SSC and HSC from Viqarunnisa Noon School and College. Prome joined BRAC University as a full-time Lecturer at the Department of Computer Science and Engineering in May 2024. Prior to this, she served as an Adjunct Lecturer in the same department for four semesters. During her undergraduate life, she served as a Student Tutor for almost two years. Her research interests include, but are not limited to, machine learning, deep learning and image processing.

## Md. Jahin Alam

- **Source URL**: https://cse.bracu.ac.bd/faculty_profile/52/md_jahin_alam

### Profile Details

- **Name**: Md. Jahin Alam
- **Designation**: Lecturer
- **Email**: jahin.alam@bracu.ac.bd

### Biography

Md. Jahin Alam joined BRAC University as a Lecturer in the Department of Computer Science and Engineering (CSE) on June 2023. He is currently pursuing his M.Sc. degree in Electrical and Electronic Engineering (EEE) with a focus on Medical Image and Signal Processing at Bangladesh University of Engineering and Technology (BUET). He acquired his B.Sc. degree from EEE, BUET on May 2022. His higher secondary journey began at Notre Dame College (NDC) in science; whereas he completed his primary and secondary school in Engineering University School and College (EUSC).

As Jahin finalized his M.Sc. studies, his research interests lie in the field of biomedical image and signal processing, specifically focusing on computer vision, segmentation strategies, prediction models, and diagnostic tools. Currently, he aims to better understand Ultrasound image reconstruction techniques as well as develop methods (signal/image processing & deep learning based) that can outperform conventional approaches in quality measures.

He is currently pursuing his PhD at Johns Hopkins University.

### Office Location / Address

- CSE Department
- 4th floor, Room No # 4M107,
- Brac University,
- Kha 224 Bir Uttam Rafiqul Islam Avenue,
- Merul Badda, Dhaka, Bangladesh
- Md. Sabbir Ahmed is a Senior Lecturer in the Department of Computer Science and Engineering at BRAC University, Dhaka, Bangladesh. His academic work spans Data Science, Machine Learning, Explainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI), Computer Vision, and Human-Computer Interaction (HCI), with a focus on developing trustworthy, interpretable, and human-centered intelligent systems. He has contributed to multiple peer-reviewed international publications and actively collaborates on interdisciplinary research initiatives.

### Research Interest

1. Human-Centered Computing and Human-Computer Interaction (HCI)
2. Explainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI) and Trustworthy AI
3. Data Science for Socio-Technical Systems
4. Usable Security and Privacy
5. Misinformation, Digital Harm, and Responsible AI Systems
6. Computer Vision
7. Machine Learning

### Biography

**Md. Sabbir Ahmed** is a Senior Lecturer in the Department of Computer Science and Engineering at **BRAC University**, Dhaka, Bangladesh. His academic work spans **Data Science, Machine Learning, Explainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI), Computer Vision, and Human-Computer Interaction (HCI)**, with a focus on developing trustworthy, interpretable, and human-centered intelligent systems. He has contributed to multiple peer-reviewed international publications and actively collaborates on interdisciplinary research initiatives.

In addition to his research and teaching, he is actively engaged in academic leadership, research community building, and student mentorship. He contributes to undergraduate thesis supervision, faculty and student engagement initiatives, and the coordination of academic and professional programs involving students, faculty members, and external partners. Through his work, he is committed to strengthening research culture and fostering an inclusive, forward-looking academic environment.

### Biography

Farah Al Fahim is a Lecturer in the Department of Computer Science and Engineering at Brac University. He previously worked remotely at a Canadian Company, AIDocBuilder Inc., where he was primarily responsible for extracting and parsing valuable data from the commercial documents of different multinational companies including DHL, Ford Motors, Airbus, Volkswagen, Hitachi, Jaguar Land Rover, etc to name a few. His research interest lies at the intersection of Human-Computer Interaction, Machine Learning, Data Structures and Algorithms, Image Processing, and Computer Vision.

## Jawaril Munshad Abedin

- **Source URL**: https://cse.bracu.ac.bd/faculty_profile/57/jawaril_munshad_abedin

### Profile Details

- **Name**: Jawaril Munshad Abedin
- **Designation**: Lecturer
- **Email**: jawaril.munshad@bracu.ac.bd

### Biography

Jawaril Munshad Abedin completed her BSc. from Brac University in Fall 2021 with a cgpa 3.99/4.00. She completed her SSC and HSC from Viqarunnisa Noon School and College in 2015 and 2017. She was also awarded the Best Girl Gold Medal from the Main Morning branch of Viqarunnisa Noon School and College in 2014. 

Jawaril joined BRAC University as a Lecturer in the Department of Computer Science and Engineering in June 2023. Prior to this, she served as a contractual lecturer in the same department for 3 semesters. She has also served as a Student Tutor for almost 2 years in the same department during her undergraduate years. 

Moreover, she served as the Vice Chairperson of the IEEE Computer Society BRAC University Student Branch Chapter in 2020. She became the first BRAC University student to win the Richard E. Merwin Student Scholarship from the IEEE Computer Society, USA in 2020 for her excellent academic result and volunteering contribution to IEEE.

* **Preprint:**

1. bbOCR: An Open-source Multi-domain OCR Pipeline for Bengali Documents. **Link: https://doi.org/10.48550/arXiv.2308.10647**

### Biography

**Aiman Jabeen Ramisa** has joined BRAC University as a contractual lecturer in the Department of Computer Science and Engineering as of October 2021. Prior to this, she has also served as a Student Tutor for 2 years in the same department during her undergraduate years. Additionally, she served as the Treasurer of IEEE BRAC University Student Branch in 2020. 

Aiman is currently pursuing her Master of Computer Science degree from Rice University, Texas. She has completed her bachelors in Computer Science and Engineering with a minor concentration in Business from BRAC University in Spring 2021. She completed her O’Levels (2014) and A’Levels (2016) from Maple Leaf International School under the Edexcel Board. Her research interests include Data Science, Machine Learning, Bioinformatics, Artificial Intelligence, Natural Language Processing.

### Office Location / Address

- 4th floor, Room No # 4G05,

- Dewan Ziaul Karim (DZK) is a Senior Lecturer in the Department of Computer Science and Engineering (CSE) at BRAC University, Dhaka, Bangladesh. He completed his M.Sc. in Computer Science and Engineering from BRAC University, having initially started the program at Bangladesh University of Engineering and Technology (BUET). After completing his coursework at BUET, he transferred his credits to BRAC University to conduct his thesis and successfully earn his degree. He holds a B.Sc. in CSE from Ahsanullah University of Science and Technology (AUST).

### Research Interest

* Computer Vision
* Image Processing
* Machine Learning

### Biography

**Dewan Ziaul Karim (DZK)** is a Senior Lecturer in the Department of Computer Science and Engineering (CSE) at BRAC University, Dhaka, Bangladesh. He completed his **M.Sc. in Computer Science and Engineering** from BRAC University, having initially started the program at Bangladesh University of Engineering and Technology (BUET). After completing his coursework at BUET, he transferred his credits to BRAC University to conduct his thesis and successfully earn his degree. He holds a **B.Sc. in CSE** from Ahsanullah University of Science and Technology (AUST).

Before joining BRAC University as a lecturer, Mr. Karim served as a full-time lecturer at **Daffodil International University** and as a part-time lecturer at **Ahsanullah University of Science and Technology**. He currently also serves as the **coordinator of the ITEE training program** at BRAC University, where he is responsible for scheduling training sessions and organizing seminars.

At BRAC University, he is currently teaching the **Digital Logic Design** theory and lab courses. Over the years, he has taught several undergraduate courses including **Introduction to Computer Science**, **Data Communications**, **Database Management Systems**, **Software Engineering**, and **System Analysis and Design**. He also regularly supervises final-year thesis and project work.

Mr. Karim’s primary research interests lie in **computer vision** and **machine learning**, with applications in **medical imaging**, **agriculture**, and **social impact sectors**. He is driven by the goal of advancing visual recognition systems through deep learning and solving real-world problems with innovative, practical solutions. His long-term vision is to contribute to research that meaningfully impacts society and inspires future generations of engineers.

**At a Glance:**

### Prospective Thesis/Project Students

Assalamualaikum.

I am looking for hardworking and sincere students for thesis/projects in Spring 2025. I primarily work in the field of applied computer vision. Likewise, I am willing to recruit students who are interested in applied computer vision or computer vision related fields. Alternatively, I am also willing to listen to any ideas that fall under the domain of machine learning. If you are interested to work with me, please feel free to email me at ziaul.karim@bracu.ac.bd. Alternatively, you can also meet me during office hours at Room#**4G05**. I am available for both supervision and co-supervision.

It is better if you come with a group of 4-5 people. But I don't have any issues with fewer people if you have a strong desire to work hard for your thesis.

**Note for students:**

Please concentrate on the “hardworking” part. You may not know everything, in fact, you may know nothing when you meet me. But you have to be willing to learn and follow instructions. Most of the time, you will be self-learning, meaning, I won't do the “learning” part for you. I will give you guidelines, though. You will have to follow and learn accordingly.

If you plan to email me, please write “Summer 2026” in your subject. This will help me to verify whether you went through this page or not.

## Dr. Amitabha Chakrabarty

- **Source URL**: https://cse.bracu.ac.bd/faculty_profile/69/dr_amitabha_chakrabarty

### Profile Details

- **Name**: Dr. Amitabha Chakrabarty

### Research Interest

I'm interested in working in the Internet of Things, Machine Learning, Deep Learning, Computer Vision, LLM models, and Networking. Some of my recent published works have predominantly revolved around leveraging deep learning techniques in various domains such as medical imaging, agriculture, and more. Students who want to collaborate with me can get involved in some of the existing ongoing research projects or propose their ideas on the domains mentioned above for independent, undergraduate, or postgraduate research.

Some of the recent publications from my team:

1. https://ieeexplore.ieee.org/document/10055787/
2. <https://ieeexplore.ieee.org/document/10100703/>
3. <https://ieeexplore.ieee.org/document/10152110/>
4. <https://ieeexplore.ieee.org/document/9532572/>
5. <https://www.mdpi.com/1424-8220/23/7/3751>
6. <https://ieeexplore.ieee.org/document/10100703>
7. <https://www.mdpi.com/2306-5354/10/1/99>
8. <https://www.hindawi.com/journals/jhe/2022/1122536/>

### Biography

**Professor Chakrabarty** currently holds the position of a Computer Science and Engineering Professor at Brac University. Previously, he worked at the Independent University, Bangladesh (IUB), overseeing IT infrastructures. His educational journey includes a Ph.D. earned from Dublin City University (DCU) in 2012, complemented by B.Sc. and M.Sc. degrees from the University of Rajshahi's Department of Computer Science and Engineering. Additionally, he pursued a second M.Sc. in Telecommunication Engineering at Independent University, Bangladesh.

His research interests span diverse areas such as the Internet of Things (IoT), Machine Learning, Computer Vision, Computer Networking, Embedded Systems, and Switching Theory. With a prolific publication record of over 100 research papers across national and international journals and conferences, Professor Chakrabarty contributes significantly to his field.

Beyond academia, he actively participates in various project consultations in development sectors. He also serves as a jury member in national IT competitions and serves as a reviewer for various international conferences and journals.

### Professional Activity

**April 5-16, 2023**  
While participating in the "International Faculty Exchange Program (IFEP)" at Chandigarh University, I taught undergraduate, postgraduate, and Ph.D. students. I also had the opportunity to engage in open discussions with faculty members regarding current research topics in computer science and shared my insights on research approaches. Furthermore, I actively participated in strategic meetings where I provided input toward the design of the Bachelor of Computer Application curriculum. It is worth noting that Chandigarh University made an impressive debut in the 2023 QS World Rankings, securing a rank of 801-1000 and becoming the youngest university in the world to achieve such a feat.  
**December 18-24, 2016**  
As part of the Erasmus+ ICM program, I participated in a faculty exchange program at Middle East Technical University (METU) in Ankara, Turkey. METU is globally recognized as one of the top universities in Europe. I was the sole candidate selected from Bangladesh and spent one week as a visiting faculty member in the Computer Engineering School. During my time at METU, I had the opportunity to present my research to both undergraduate students and faculty members.

## Md. Ahasanul Alam

- **Source URL**: https://cse.bracu.ac.bd/faculty_profile/82/md_ahasanul_alam

### Profile Details

- **Name**: Md. Ahasanul Alam
- **Designation**: Lecturer
- **Email**: ahasanul.alam@bracu.ac.bd

### Office Location / Address

- CSE Department
- 4th floor, Room No # 4M133,
- Brac University,
- Kha 224 Bir Uttam Rafiqul Islam Avenue,
- Merul Badda, Dhaka, Bangladesh
- Md. Ahasanul Alam worked at BRAC University as a Lecturer till 2025. He received his bachelor's degree in Computer Science and Engineering from the University of Dhaka, Bangladesh, and is currently pursuing a master's degree at the same institution. Previously, he worked as a Research Assistant (RA) with the Cognitive Agents and Interaction Laboratory (CAIL), University of Dhaka. His research interests include Multi-Agent Systems, Multi-Agent Coordination, Multi-Agent Path Finding (MAPF), and Natural Language Processing (NLP).[Here is my CV](https://drive.google.com/file/d/1GBZ1bqYJ3sYlK8n01_M_0XYz9Gb3FyOM/view?usp=sharing)

### Research Interest

**Multi Agent Path Finding**  
The Multi-Agent Pathfinding (MAPF) problem is the fundamental problem of planning paths for multiple agents, where the key constraint is that the agents will be able to follow these paths concurrently without colliding with each other. Research on MAPF has been flourishing in the past couple of years. Although a MAPF problem needs to be solved for many real-world deployments, solving such a problem optimally is NP-hard. Consequently, the main focus is to find sub-optimal algorithms to improve scalability and solution runtime.

**Course Related**: Data Structure and Algorithms, Discrete Mathematics, Numerical Methods, Artificial Intelligence  
  
  
**Natural Language Processing**

Natural language processing (NLP) is an interdisciplinary subfield of linguistics, computer science, and artificial intelligence concerned with the interactions between computers and human language, in particular how to program computers to process and analyze large amounts of natural language data. Although several works have been done in recent years on English and other languages, scope for improvement in Bangla NLP is still very wide. Efficient techniques would help computers to communicate with humans in their own language and scales other language-related tasks.   
  
**Course Related:** Algorithms, Numerical Methods, Artificial Intelligence, Neural Networks, Machine Learning, Linear Algebra, Probability and Statistics

### Biography

**Md. Ahasanul Alam** worked at BRAC University as a Lecturer till 2025. He received his bachelor's degree in Computer Science and Engineering from the University of Dhaka, Bangladesh, and is currently pursuing a master's degree at the same institution. Previously, he worked as a Research Assistant (RA) with the Cognitive Agents and Interaction Laboratory (CAIL), University of Dhaka. His research interests include Multi-Agent Systems, Multi-Agent Coordination, Multi-Agent Path Finding (MAPF), and Natural Language Processing (NLP).  
[Here is my CV](https://drive.google.com/file/d/1GBZ1bqYJ3sYlK8n01_M_0XYz9Gb3FyOM/view?usp=sharing)

* M. T. Alam, M. A. Alam, M. M. Rahman, and M. M. Khan, "Rel-HNN: Split Parallel Hypergraph Neural Network for Learning on Relational Databases," *Transactions on Machine Learning Research*, 2025.

### Profile Details

### Biography

Jishnu Mahmud is currently a lecturer in the Department of Computer Science and Engineering under the School of Data and Sciences at BRAC University. He completed his undergraduate degree from the Department of Electrical and Electronic Engineering, majoring in Communication and Signal Processing, at Bangladesh University of Engineering and Technology.

He is an enthusiastic researcher in quantum information (including but not limited to quantum error correction, tomography, and quantum variational algorithms).

In addition to his academic interests, Jishnu is a classical guitarist at the Classical Music Academy of Dhaka and can be seen taking his film cameras out for walks now and then.

### Biography

**Purbayan Das** is an enthusiastic educator and researcher who has been a lecturer at BRAC University's Department of CSE since January 2023. He graduated in 2021 with a Bachelor of Science in EEE from Bangladesh University of Engineering and Technology, where he is currently pursuing a Master of Science in the same discipline. His passion for electronics prompted him to do research in the field of nanoelectronics, namely nanophotonics. His research interests include nanophotonics, optoelectronics, and neuromorphic computing, as well as their applications in other fields. He loves to be actively engaged in activities linked to problem-solving and research, constantly stretching the limits of his knowledge and abilities. In addition, he is an avid sports fan who used to regularly compete in hall and departmental sports events during his undergraduate studies. As part of a recent CSE Outdoor Team Building and Workshop, he also participated in a friendly football tournament among coworkers, which his team won.  
  
Purbayan has always had a passion for teaching, and he considers himself fortunate to be able to pursue this passion professionally. He is able to pique his audience's interest in pondering unfamiliar ideas and looking at familiar things in novel ways, that make him well suited for teaching. Driven by his enthusiasm for teaching, he has been regularly producing high quality instructional materials for both teachers and students since joining this university, demonstrating his competence, capacity, and commitment to this profession.  
  
He has honed his leadership skills throughout his school, college, and professional life. As the coordinator for the Circuits and Electronics (CSE250) course, he was given the opportunity to modify the course syllabi to better meet the needs of students. He enjoys playing cricket, football, and badminton in his spare time, as well as traveling and watching movies. As a person, he is best described by intuition, compassion, prospectivity, generosity, and idealism.

### CSE250 Course Material

Course General Information
==========================

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| **Course Code:** | CSE250  CSE250L |
| **Course Title:** | Circuits and Electronics  Circuits and Electronics Laboratory |
| **Credit Hours**(Theory+Lab): | 3 + 0 |
| **Contact Hours**(Theory+Lab): | 3 + 3 |
| **Category:** | Program Core |
| **Type:** | Required, Engineering, Lecture + Laboratory |
| **Prerequisites:** | None |
| **Co-requisites:** | None |

Course Contents
===============

Fundamental electrical concepts and measuring units of electrical charge, voltage, current, resistance, and power; Laws of electricity (Ohm's law, Kirchhoff's Current and Voltage law) and various methods of electrical circuit analysis (Nodal, Mesh); Introduction to basic electrical circuit elements; I-V characteristics; Circuit analysis in Direct current, First-order Transient and Alternating current mode, for various combinations of Resistive, Inductive and Capacitive networks;  Phasor representation of sinusoidal quantities; Circuit theorems for linear circuits (Source Transformation, Superposition, Thevenin, Norton and Maximum Power Transfer); Open loop and closed loop configuration of Operational Amplifiers. 

* **This course includes compulsory 3-hour laboratory work.**

### Projects And Researches

**Predicting Peak Performance of a Cricket Player Using Machine Learning and Data Analytics** | Undergraduate Thesis, 2021 – 2022

* Analyzed commentary from match statistics and formulated new parameters to generate corpus dataset; Implemented regression and classification algorithms and compared performance
* Corpus dataset: <https://tinyurl.com/odi-batter-dataset>; <https://tinyurl.com/odi-bowler-dataset>;

**Prediction of Parkinson's Disease from Voice Measurements using Machine Learning Classification Algorithms** | 2022

* Used voice signals dataset from UCI machine learning repository and implemented machine learning to make models for detecting Parkinson’s disease; Model performance was based on having low false negatives from the confusion matrix
* Preprint: <http://dx.doi.org/10.13140/RG.2.2.11742.61761>

**Tele Net App Service** | 2022

* Networking system using six intermediary devices as branches and end devices; IP assignment using VLSM based on host requirement; Simulated using Cisco Packet Tracer
* Source: <https://github.com/ExGranite/tele-net-app-service>

**Art Gallery Management** | 2022

* System analysis and design using use case diagram, activity diagram, sequence diagram, data flow diagram and windows navigation diagram
* Source: <https://github.com/ExGranite/ArtGalleryManagement>

**Smart Trash Can** | 2022

* Smart trash can system using HC-SR04 ultrasonic sensor, active infrared obstacle sensor and Arduino Uno R3; ultrasonic sensor determines the amount of available space inside and the infrared sensor automates the opening of the trash can; Simulated using Proteus and LabVIEW
* Source: <https://github.com/ExGranite/smart-trash-can>

**Data Logger** | 2022

* Data logger system using DHT11 and Arduino Uno R3; DHT11 reads the temperature and humidity readings and gives a visual representation; Simulated using Proteus and LabVIEW
* Source: <https://github.com/ExGranite/data-logger>

**Online Food Ordering System** | 2021

* A software engineering project based on Laravel 8; Languages: php, html, css, javascript
* Source: <https://github.com/ExGranite/OnlineFoodOrderingSystem>

**Line Follower Robot** | 2021

* PID implemented line-following robot using an infrared sensor to read a black line and follow it; Simulated using Webots from Cyberbotics; Language: python
* Source: <https://github.com/ExGranite/line-follower>

**Obstacle Avoiding Robot** | 2021

* Obstacle-avoiding and object-detection robot using distance sensors and camera sensors; Simulated using Webots from cyberbotics; Language: python
* Source: <https://github.com/ExGranite/obstacle-avoiding>

### About Myself

I am Warida Rashid, currently working as a lecturer in Computer Science and Engineering at Brac University. I have graduated from the same university as the Valedictorian and the Chancellor's Gold Medalist of the 12th convocation. I also have studied Architecture for three years at BUET before deciding that I want to pursue something that is better suited for my intellectual attributes.

I have been working here since 2018 as a lecturer. In addition to that, I led a team that handled academic complaints from the students. I also worked as an Assistant Proctor for a while.

I am interested in Psychology and technology-driven interventions to pave the path for a society that is safe, healthy, and functional. I seek to work with people who are intuitive and understand the big picture while learning and solving problems.

## Dr. Md. Golam Rabiul Alam

- **Source URL**: https://cse.bracu.ac.bd/faculty_profile/113/dr_md_golam_rabiul_alam

### Profile Details

- **Name**: Dr. Md. Golam Rabiul Alam

### Research Interest

* Data Science & Big-data Analytics
* Machine Learning, Deep Learning, and Reinforcement Learning
* Health informatics, Edge computing, and Medical Image Processing
* Affective Computing, Mental Health, and Well-being

### Biography

Md. Golam Rabiul Alam is a Professor at the Computer Science and Engineering Department of BRAC University. He received Ph.D. in Computer Engineering from Kyung Hee University, South Korea in 2017. He served as a Post-doctoral researcher in Computer Science and Engineering Department, at Kyung Hee University, Korea from March 2017 to February 2018. Dr. Rabiul Alam received B.S. and M.S. degrees in Computer Science and Engineering, and Information Technology from Khulna University and University of Dhaka respectively.   Dr. Rabiul Alam has published around 170 research articles in reputed journals, and conference proceedings. He has also 3 registered patents on ambient assisted living, mobile cloud computing, and mobile fog computing, respectively. Dr. Rabiul Alam's research interests include Mental-health informatics, Ambient intelligence systems, Mobile-cloud computing, Edge computing, Affective computing, and UAV Image processing.  He is a member of the IEEE Computer Society and received several best paper awards at national and international research conferences.

## Meem Arafat Manab

- **Source URL**: https://cse.bracu.ac.bd/faculty_profile/114/meem_arafat_manab

### Profile Details

- **Name**: Meem Arafat Manab
- **Designation**: Lecturer
- **Email**: meem.arafat@bracu.ac.bd
- **Contact / Ext**: +353851522813

### Biography

Meem Arafat Manab is a Lecturer (on leave) in the Department of Computer Science and Engineering at BRAC University (active period: 2020-2023), where they taught several courses, including artificial intelligence, simulation and modeling, and geospatial data analysis, and managed the OSUN Science Shop programme with Central European University, European Humanities University, and American University of Central Asia. They now continue their academic career as a Marie Skłodowska-Curie Doctoral Fellow at the Ontology Engineering Group, Universidad Politécnica de Madrid, researching how legal obligations can be made machine-readable without losing the objectives of the law, as part of the HARNESS Doctoral Network funded by the Horizon Europe Project.

Manab holds a European Master's in Law, Data and AI (EMILDAI) from Dublin City University and Universidad de León, and a BSc in Computer Science and Engineering from the University of Dhaka. They also hold a Research Associate (pro bono) position at Trinity College Dublin's School of Religion, Theology and Peace Studies. Meem is a Visiting Researcher at IDLab, Ghent University, imec, and CIRSFID, University of Bologna, and sits on the European Data Protection Board's Support Pool of Experts and The Copyright Society's AI Subcommittee.

- **Designation**: Lecturer
- **Email**: sayeed.mohammad@bracu.ac.bd
- **Websites**: Available on portal

### Biography

I am currently appointed in the position of lecturer, Department of Computer Science and Engineering at BRAC University from June 2022. I have completed my B.Sc. (2022) from the Department of Electrical and Electronic Engineering, Bangladesh University of Engineering and Technology.

I am highly interested in research in the fields of perception and planning of autonomous mobile, aerial, bipedal and quadrupedal robots, and robotic manipulators. Being an avid fan of robotics and automation, I enjoy designing automatic machines and watching them come to life. I believe that the unification of robotics and AI is the path toward a new future. During my undergraduate studies, I completed several projects related to electrical engineering, robotics, and computer vision. I have also conducted several workshops related to embedded systems and the basics of robotics.

- CSE Department
- 4th floor, Room No # 4G12,
- Brac University,
- Kha 224 Bir Uttam Rafiqul Islam Avenue,
- Merul Badda, Dhaka, Bangladesh

### Research Interest

[Machine Learning](https://scholar.google.com/citations?view_op=search_authors&hl=en&mauthors=label:machine_learning), [Deep Learning and](https://scholar.google.com/citations?view_op=search_authors&hl=en&mauthors=label:deep_learning) [Computer Vision](https://scholar.google.com/citations?view_op=search_authors&hl=en&mauthors=label:computer_vision).

### Biography

Hello!   
  
I am Saiful Islam, someone who is passionate about teaching and is currently enrolled as a Senior lecturer at BRAC University. As a dedicated mentor and educator, my teaching dynamics revolves around innovation, integrity, and inclusivity. My love for teaching has transcended classrooms and has been depicted on platforms like YouTube (<https://www.youtube.com/@saifulcreates>) which I created for the sole purpose of inspiring keen learners around the world. Through my tutorials and study materials, I  hope to live my dreams through my students and ensure education is accessible to all.   
  
**Research Area(s):** [Google scholar profile](https://scholar.google.com/citations?view_op=list_works&hl=en&hl=en&user=w5zaewYAAAAJ&sortby=title)   
  
I am wholeheartedly dedicated to nurture my students to achieve global milestones. My unwavering commitment behind the success of my students has earned me accolades like a Gold Medal at the International Science and Invention Fair organized by the Young Scientists Association. Besides, I have served as a Co-Principal Investigator at BRACU Mongol Tori and was selected to participate in NASA’s Mars Rover Competition that took place in Utah, USA. Additionally, I have served as the Co-Advisor of the Robotics Club. Together, all these experiences make me want to foster innovation and creativity amongst all students.   
  
At the same time, my leadership as a team leader of Techniche, a fully-funded Techno-Management Festival at the Indian Institute of Technology (IIT), showcases my organizational acumen and effective leadership qualities. This role contributed to securing a merit position at the prestigious Indian Rover Challenge (IRC), organized by Vellore Institute of Technology (VIT), Chennai, India. Apart from the above mentioned, my other contributions have been featured in several prominent news sources, for example - [Mars Society South Asia](https://southasia.marssociety.org/irc-2018/), [The Daily Star,](https://www.thedailystar.net/tech-startup/news/team-atlas-bangladesh-reaches-finals-australian-rover-challenge-2024-3582541) [BRACU Express](https://dspace.bracu.ac.bd/xmlui/bitstream/handle/10361/11302/BRACU%20Express_Volume%201_May%2028%2C%202017.pdf?sequence=1&isAllowed=y).

## Dr. Aniqua Nusrat Zereen

### Research Interest

Human Analytics

* Blind Assistive Systems
* Eldercare Systems

### Biography

Aniqua Zereen completed her PhD in Computer Science from Asian Institute of Technology, Thailand, in 2023. She worked as a Research Assistant at the Artificial Intelligence Center at AIT for over 2 years and successfully completed a national project for 'NBTC' in Thailand. She also worked as a co-investigator for a project at Mahidol University, Thailand. Aniqua is a recipient of the Bangabandhu Science and Technology Fellowship Trust. Before joining AIT as a PhD student, she served as a Lecturer in the Department of Computer Science and Engineering at BRAC University from 2013 to 2017. Additionally, she worked as a summer student at CERN, the European Organization for Nuclear Research, in 2013.

## Prantik Paul

- **Source URL**: https://cse.bracu.ac.bd/faculty_profile/129/prantik_paul

### Profile Details

- **Name**: Prantik Paul
- **Designation**: Lecturer
- **Email**: prantik.paul@bracu.ac.bd
- **Websites**: Available on portal

### Biography

Prantik Paul is a highly accomplished individual currently serving as a Lecturer in the Department of Computer Science and Engineering at Brac University. With an outstanding academic background and a plethora of achievements, he has made remarkable contributions to the field.

Prantik's educational journey is marked by excellence. He obtained his Secondary School Certificate (SSC) from Chittagong Collegiate School in Chittagong, securing the first position in the Talentpool Scholarship Merit List with a perfect GPA of 5.00/5.00. Continuing his success, he pursued his Higher Secondary Certificate (HSC) from Chittagong College, where he once again secured the top position in the Talentpool Scholarship Merit List with an impeccable GPA of 5.00/5.00.

His passion for computer science and engineering led Prantik to pursue a Bachelor of Science degree in Computer Science and Engineering from Bangladesh University of Engineering and Technology (BUET). With unwavering dedication, he maintained a remarkable CGPA of 3.9214/4.00, showcasing his commitment to academic excellence.

Prantik's research experience is extensive, with a notable project being his undergraduate thesis on Common Sense based Question Answering. Under the guidance of Dr. Rifat Shahriyar, he introduced the concept of Bangla ConceptNet, showcasing his expertise in natural language processing and problem-solving.

Throughout his academic journey, Prantik undertook several exceptional projects, each highlighting his proficiency in various programming languages, frameworks, and tools. Notably, he developed a Heart Condition Classifier using deep learning techniques, employed PyTorch and TensorFlow frameworks in a project on Bangla Digit Recognition implemented from scratch, and contributed to the development of COMPASS, a recommendation system software utilizing Node.js, Express.js, and MongoDB.

In addition to his academic accomplishments, Prantik has received numerous awards and prizes. He has consistently achieved the Dean's List Award at BUET, acknowledging his outstanding performance and academic achievements. He has also been a recipient of multiple scholarships, including the Talentpool Scholarship at both the Secondary and Higher Secondary levels. His consistent first positions in the scholarship merit lists reflect his exceptional abilities and dedication to learning.

Prantik has excelled in various regional olympiads, securing top positions in competitions such as the Bangladesh Math Olympiad (Regional), Bangladesh Science Olympiad (Regional), Bangladesh Zoology Olympiad (Regional), and Bangladesh Chemistry Olympiad (Regional). These accolades underline his exceptional problem-solving skills and aptitude in STEM fields.

Beyond academics, Prantik has pursued his passions and interests. He has completed an 8-year music course as a vocalist at Sunrise Sangit Niketon in Chittagong. Additionally, he has honed his skills in recitation and received training in art at Sowkat Jahan Art School, winning several prizes in exhibitions.

Prantik's achievements extend beyond his personal pursuits. He has actively participated in extracurricular activities and served in various leadership roles. As the Organizational Secretary (Logistics) at Murchhona, the leading cultural club in BUET, he demonstrated his organizational skills and commitment to promoting cultural activities. He has also been an Executive Member of BFS (BUET Film Society) and Shopnosarothi, a humanitarian organization that focuses on scholarships, relief programs during the pandemic, and assisting those in need.

With his extensive skills in programming languages, frameworks, and databases, Prantik possesses a solid foundation in data science, machine learning, and software development. His ability to leverage Python, C, C++, Java, and a range of frameworks including Node.js, Express.js, React, and PyTorch, among others, exemplify his versatility and adaptability in various technological domains.

In summary, Prantik Paul is a dedicated individual with a passion for computer science and engineering, consistently achieving academic excellence and garnering recognition through awards and scholarships. His research contributions, impressive project portfolio, and active involvement in extracurricular activities reflect his well-rounded abilities and commitment to personal and professional growth.

### Professional Activity

Prantik Paul has been teaching as an lecturer in Brac University, Department of Computer Science and Engineering. He is currently an instructor of CSE220 (Data Structure), CSE220L, CSE230 (Discrete Mathematics) and CSE420L (Compiler)

### Awards

→ Awarded Dean's List Award in each term in BUET.

## Raisa Mashtura

- **Source URL**: https://cse.bracu.ac.bd/faculty_profile/131/raisa_mashtura

### Profile Details

- **Name**: Raisa Mashtura

### Biography

Raisa Mashtura is a lecturer at the Department of Computer Science and Engineering under the School of Data and Sciences of BRAC University. She completed her undergraduate degree in Electrical and Electronics Engineering, majoring in Communication and Signal Processing from the Bangladesh University of Engineering and Technology. Her current research interest includes Digital Signal Processing, Computer Vision & Quantum Machine Learning.

Alongside her academics, she pursues interests in music, embroidery, indulging in Agatha Christie novels, and in culinary arts.

### Publication

* [Quantum Convolutional Neural Networks with Interaction Layers for Classification of Classical Data](https://doi.org/10.1007/s42484-024-00145-4) - Mahmud, J., Mashtura, R., Fattah, S.A. et al., Quantum Machine Intelligence 6, 11 (2024)

### Biography

Priata Nowshin enrolled at BRAC University to study Computer Science in 2018, after completing her S.S.C. and H.S.C. from Viqarunnisa Noon School and College. During her bachelor's degree, she worked as a Student Tutor at her university, offering academic assistance to students under her supervision. In October 2021, she received her degree with honors from the Computer Science and Engineering Department of BRAC University. The following semester, she joined her alumni university to pursue a lifelong dream of teaching and educating young people.

## Dr. Chowdhury Mofizur Rahman

- **Source URL**: https://cse.bracu.ac.bd/faculty_profile/151/dr_chowdhury_mofizur_rahman

### Profile Details

- **Name**: Dr. Chowdhury Mofizur Rahman

### Research Interest

All slots are occupied till spring 2027

### Biography

Professor Dr.  Chowdhury Mofizur Rahman did his Ph.D in Machine Learning back in 1996 from Tokyo Institute of Technology, one of the finest institutions of Japan. He did his Masters and Bachelors from BUET in 1992 and 1989 respectively. He served BUET as a faculty of CSE Department for about 15 years and during his tenure in BUET, he served the CSE department as the head from 1999 to 2001. After taking early retirement from BUET, in 2003 he founded United International University (UIU) with the financial patronage of United Group. He worked there as a Pro VC for about 15 years and he served the same institution as the Vice Chancellor from 2018 to 2022. On 15th May, 2023 he started working for BRAC University as a professor of CSE Department in the school of Data and Sciences. After working at BRACU for one trimester, he joined State University of Bangladesh (SUB) as Vice Chancellor (Designate). After serving SUB one year, Professor Rahman came back to BRACU CSE department and since then he has been working at BRACU as a professor of CSE department till to date.

### Professional Activity

* Head, CSE Department, BUET from 1999 to 2001
* Pro Vice Chancellor from 2006 to 2010, 2010 t0 2014 and 2014 to 2018 of United International University
* Vice Chancellor from 2018 to 2022 of United International University
* Member, BdREN Trust, University Grants Commission of Bangladesh from February 2020 to March, 2022

* Worked as coordinator from Bangladesh in the EU sponsored (EM ECW Ref: 149674-EM-1-2008-1-UK-ERAMUNDUS) eLINK (*e*ast west *L*ink for *I*nnovation, *N*etworking and *K*nowledge exchange) project. In this project five host EU institutions invited third country students, teachers and researchers to carry out 10 months study/research activities in their premises. These institutions are University of Bradford, UK, Staffordshire University, UK, Corvinus University of Budapest, Hungary, University of Sannio, Italy and University LUMIERE LYON2, France. The Asian partners who took part in the project are United International University, Bangladesh, College of Science and Technology, Bhutan, Kantipur Engineering College, Nepal, Mohammed Ali Jinnah University, Pakistan, Shanghai Jiao Tong University, China and Chiang Mai University, Thailand.  From a total of 334 scholarships worth of 5.5 million Euro, Bangladesh enjoyed 85 scholarships under undergraduate, masters, ph.d, post doctoral and teachers training categories.

* Worked as joint coordinator in the EU sponsored Erasmus Mundus Action 2 partnerships (372242-1-2012-1-UK-ERA MUNDUS –EMA 21) Clink ( Centre of Excellence for Learning, Innovation, Networking and Knowledge) project.  In this project six host EU institutions invited third country students, teachers and researchers to carry out 10/20 months study/research activities in their premises. These institutions are University of Northumbria at Newcastle, UK, Staffordshire University, UK, Corvinus University of Budapest, Hungary, University of Bremen, Germany and University LUMIERE LYON2, France and Gheorghe Asachi Technical University of Lasi, Romania. The Asian partners who took part in the project are United International University, Bangladesh, Royal University, Bhutan, Kantipur Engineering College, Nepal, Mohammed Ali Jinnah University, Pakistan, Jadavpur University, India, Northeastern University, China and Chiang Mai University, Thailand and University Putra, Malaysia.  From a total of 112 scholarships, Bangladesh enjoyed 22 scholarships under undergraduate, masters, ph.d, post doctoral and teachers training categories.

* Worked as joint coordinator in the EU (ERUSMUS MUNDUS) sponsored   FUSION (Featured eUrope and South asIa mObility Network) project which aims to foster partnerships of emerging Asian countries (Afghanistan, Bangladesh, Bhutan, Nepal, Pakistan, China, India, and Thailand) with the EU countries (Bulgaria, France Germany, Hungary, Italy, Portugal, Sweden, and UK) to reinforce the existing collaborations developed through the EU funded projects. In this project ten host EU institutions invite third country students, teachers and researchers to carry out 10/20 months study/research activities in their premises. Similarly EU students and researchers will carry out 6/10 months study/research in Asian universities. All the cost involved will be borne by EU. The EU partners are [Bournemouth University](http://home.bournemouth.ac.uk/), United Kingdom, Rusenski universitet “Angel Kanchev”, Bulgaria, [Université LUMIERE LYON2](http://www.univ-lyon2.fr/), France, [University of Bremen](http://www.logdynamics.com/), Germany, [Corvinus University of Budapest](http://www.uni-corvinus.hu/), Hungary, University of Sannio, Italy, Universidade de Évora, Portugal, Mälardalen University, Sweden and [University of Northumbria at Newcastle](http://www.northumbria.ac.uk/), United Kingdom. The Asian partners are Information and Communication Technology Institute, Afghanistan, Kabul University, Afghanistan, [United International University](http://www.uiu.ac.bd/), Bangladesh, College of Science and Technology, [Royal University of Bhutan](http://www.rub.edu.bt/), Bhutan, Chengdu University, China, Zhongyuan University of Technology, China, Asia Pacific Institute of Information Technology SD India, India, Gulbarga University , India, [Kantipur Engineering College](http://www.kec.edu.np/), Nepal, [Mohammed Ali Jinnah University](http://www.jinnah.edu.pk/), Islamabad, Pakistan and [Chiang Mai University](http://cmu.ac.th/), Thailand. This 48 months project is worth of 3.05 million Euro and is aimed to offer 151 scholarships in total.

Worked as an external expert member of Computer Science & Engg. Departments of

* South Asian University, New Delhi (A SAARC University)
* University of Dhaka
* University of Rajshahi
* Khulna University of Science and Technology
* Islamic University of Technology

* BRAC university

* Worked as the Moderator for Bangladesh from March, 2001 to September, 2004 to carry out quality checks on IT learning and assessment in NCC education accredited centers in Bangladesh
* Worked as an expert in the evaluation of Bangladesh Railway Reservation and Ticketing Project for the final acceptance by the Government
* Worked as a council member of Bangladesh Computer Council from 1999 to 2001

* Worked as a member of the technical committee in many national organizations like Bangladesh Shilpa Bank, Sonali Bank, AG office, Postal Department of Bangladesh, Cabinet Secretariat of the Govt. of Bangladesh and so on

### Research Interest

Natural Language Processing, Education Technology

### Biography

I am Lecturer in the Computer Science and Engineering Department of BRAC University and currently I am teaching Database Systems. I obtained my joint M.Sc. degree in ICT-Innovation from Aalto University, Finland and Technical University of Berlin, Germany in 2016. I received my B.Sc. in Computer Science and Engineering from BRAC University, Bangladesh in 2013. My Research interests include Natural Language Processing and Education Technology.  I was born in 1990 in a beautiful city called Sylhet, the largest hub of tea production estates in Bangladesh. Apart from being a full-time teacher, I love to travel, eat and occasionally read/write.

## Mr. Ankan Ghosh Dastider

- **Source URL**: https://cse.bracu.ac.bd/faculty_profile/161/mr_ankan_ghosh_dastider

### Profile Details

- **Name**: Mr. Ankan Ghosh Dastider
- **Designation**: Lecturer
- **Email**: ankan.ghosh@bracu.ac.bd
- **Websites**: Available on portal

### Biography

Ankan Ghosh Dastider is a Lecturer (currently on leave) at the Department of Computer Science and Engineering, School of Data and Sciences, BRAC University. He obtained a B.Sc. degree in Electrical and Electronic Engineering (EEE) with an Electronics major from the Bangladesh University of Engineering and Technology (BUET) in 2022. His interest to explore diverse arenas in his research career led to the publication of 4 research articles in various reputed journals and 4 conference papers so far. He is currently a member of the IEEE and IEEE EDS Society. He has actively participated in numerous synergistic and leadership activities and got selected to attend various international conferences and workshops. He strongly believes that knowledge should belong to all and has been working in a voluntary role with open knowledge projects like Wikipedia having this goal in mind. His research interest includes 2D materials, Materials property, Device physics, Nanotechnology, Data-based Materials property exploration, and Deep learning.

## Dr. Md. Ashraful Alam

- **Source URL**: https://cse.bracu.ac.bd/faculty_profile/164/dr_md_ashraful_alam

### Profile Details

- **Name**: Dr. Md. Ashraful Alam

### Research Interest

* Computer Vision
* Image Processing
* Machine Learning and Deep Learning
* CUDA Deep Neural Network (cuDNN)
* Vision-Language Models (VLMs)
* Medical Image and Data Analytics
* 3D Vision
* Machine Vision
* Advanced Color Vision for Self-driving Cars & Industrial Solutions
* Virtual and Augmented Reality

### Biography

**Dr. Md. Ashraful Alam** is working as an Associate Professor and the Director of CVIS Research Lab (Computer Vision and Intelligent Systems) at the Department of Computer Science and Engineering in BRAC University. Prior to joining BRAC University, Dr. Alam was working as a Post-Doc. at the School of Information and Communication Engineering in Chungbuk National University, South Korea. He received his Doctor of Engineering degree in Computer and Communication Engineering from the same university in February 2015. During his PhD program, he had been awarded “BK21 Best Student Award” in 2014 for his outstanding achievements as a BK21 scholar. Dr. Alam has published more than 100 papers in reputed international journals and conference proceedings. Dr. Alam's research interests include computer vision, image processing, machine learning, deep learning, machine vision, 3-D vision, medical data and image analytics, virtual and augmented reality. He is also engaged as Reviewer, Editorial Board Member, and Lead Guest Editor (Special issue) in reputed international journals.

**Google Scholar Profile**

[**https://scholar.google.com/citations?hl=en&user=-o17Jo0AAAAJ**](https://scholar.google.com/citations?hl=en&user=-o17Jo0AAAAJ)

## Ahmed Mahir Ruhan

- **Designation**: Lecturer

### Office Location / Address

- CSE Department
- 4th floor, Room No # 4L93,
- Brac University,
- Kha 224 Bir Uttam Rafiqul Islam Avenue,
- Merul Badda, Dhaka, Bangladesh
- Ahmed Mahir Ruhan is a Lecturer in the Department of Computer Science and Engineering. Formerly, he held the position of Student Tutor for 5 semesters. He completed his Bachelor of Science in Computer and Engineering from Brac University with Highest Distinction. Before joining the University as a Lecturer, Ruhan briefly served as a Cloud Solution Architect at Huawei Technologies Ltd.

### Research Interest

* Machine Learning Applications
* Deep Learning
* HCI
* Computer Vision
* Natural Language Processing

### Biography

Ahmed Mahir Ruhan is a Lecturer in the Department of Computer Science and Engineering. Formerly, he held the position of Student Tutor for 5 semesters. He completed his Bachelor of Science in Computer and Engineering from Brac University with Highest Distinction. Before joining the University as a Lecturer, Ruhan briefly served as a Cloud Solution Architect at Huawei Technologies Ltd.

His research interests lie in Natural Language Processing, Image Processing, Trend Analysis and Machine Learning Applications.

### Publication

* A Machine Learning Approach to Predict Movie Success from YouTube Trailer Comments. https://ieeexplore.ieee.org/abstract/document/10055275
