## Marshia Nujhat

- **Source URL**: https://cse.bracu.ac.bd/faculty_profile/173/marshia_nujhat

### Profile Details

- **Name**: Marshia Nujhat
- **Designation**: Lecturer
- **Email**: marshia.nujhat@bracu.ac.bd

### Office Location / Address

- CSE Department
- 4th floor, Room No # 4K85,
- Brac University,
- Kha 224 Bir Uttam Rafiqul Islam Avenue,
- Merul Badda, Dhaka, Bangladesh

### Research Interest

Human-Computer Interaction (HCI)

User-Centered Design

Social Computing

Usable Privacy and Security

### Biography

Marshia Nujhat is a Full Time Lecturer in the department of Computer Science and Engineering at BRAC University. She completed her B.Sc in Computer Science and Engineering from the same institution with the highest distinction and is additionally pursuing her M.Sc in Computer Science and Engineering here as well. Prior to being a faculty member, she served as a Student Tutor, which shaped her passion for teaching.   
  
Her research interests lie in the areas of Human-Computer Interaction (HCI), UI, UX and Usable Privacy & Security. She actively engages in research that utilizes qualitative and quantitative approaches to draw user insights on existing and emerging technologies, and build more user-centered and accessible systems. Her works also address the privacy and security concerns of digital technologies, primarily emerging from human factors and human errors. She is currently looking to explore the prospects of interactive and immersive systems in a Global South context. She is open to collaborations with researchers of similar interest.

## Dr. Mohammad Kaykobad

- **Source URL**: https://cse.bracu.ac.bd/faculty_profile/177/dr_mohammad_kaykobad

### Profile Details

- **Name**: Dr. Mohammad Kaykobad

### Research Interest

Graph Theory, Systems of Linear Equations, Computational Geometry, General Algorithms,  Ranking algorithms, Linear programming

### Biography

Professor Kaykobad served as a Distinguished Professor of the Department of Computer Science and Engineering at Brac University. He retired from CSE Department, BUET in 2020.  He was awarded the Gold Medal for contribution to ICT Education by the Bangladesh Computer Society in 2005. In 2006 he won the Bangladesh Academy of Sciences Gold Medal for Physical Sciences Senior Group. He was one of the  pioneers in introducing competitive programming in Bangladesh. He led the BUET team to the ACM Finals numerous times and his 2000 team finished 11th in the world. He received the Outstanding Coach Award at the ACM ICPC Finals held in Honolulu, Hawaii in 2002. In 2013 he was awarded the Senior Coach Award at the ACM ICPC Finals in St. Petersburg. In 2019 he was awarded ICPC Foundation Life Coach Award at Porto, Portugal. He is one of the pioneers in introducing Mathematics, Informatics and Science   Olympiads   in Bangladesh. He has worked as a Director of the Dhaka and Chittagong Stock Exchanges for many years and is a member of the Academic Councils and Syndicates of several universities. He is currently an Independent Director of Sonali Bank.

Dr. Kaykobad was a Visiting Professor at the Department of Computer Science and Engineering, the Chinese University of Hong Kong during the years 2000 and 2001, KAIST, Korea, Monash University Gippsland Campus, Australian National University, Amritapuri University, India Kyong Hee University, Korea. He is keen to popularize science and develop analytical skill in Bangladeshi students. He is a very frequent column writer inspiring educational excellence and patriotism in young people. Dr Kaykobad is a Fellow of Bangladesh Academy of Sciences.

## Dr. Sadia Hamid Kazi

- **Source URL**: https://cse.bracu.ac.bd/faculty_profile/180/dr_sadia_hamid_kazi

### Profile Details

- **Name**: Dr. Sadia Hamid Kazi

### Research Interest

Networking  
Technical Education

### Biography

**Dr. Sadia Hamid Kazi** is an Associate Professor in the Computer Science and Engineering Department under the School of Data and Sciences at Brac University. She received her Ph.D. degree in Information Systems with an emphasis on cognitive modeling.  
She has been with Brac University since 2003. Prior to joining BRAC University, she had five years of industry experience. Her research interests lie in the broad areas of Adhoc networking, network security, and cognitive modeling. She was a part of a Malaysian Government initiative under the Exploratory Research Grant Scheme (ERGS), under which she has a patent from the Malaysian IPO authority.

## Md. Saiful Bari Siddiqui

- **Source URL**: https://cse.bracu.ac.bd/faculty_profile/184/md_saiful_bari_siddiqui

### Profile Details

- **Name**: Md. Saiful Bari Siddiqui
- **Designation**: Senior Lecturer
- **Email**: saiful.bari@bracu.ac.bd
- **Websites**: Available on portal

### Office Location / Address

- CSE Department
- 4th floor, Room No # 4N152,
- Brac University,
- Kha 224 Bir Uttam Rafiqul Islam Avenue,
- Merul Badda, Dhaka, Bangladesh

### Research Interest

Machine Learning, Deep Learning, Medical Imaging, Bioinformatics, Computer Vision, Signal & Image Processing, Federated Learning.

### Biography

**Md. Saiful Bari Siddiqui** **(Iftu)** is a passionate educator and researcher with a strong background in engineering and computer science. His personality type is **ENTP-A**. His constant tendency to question the establishment and norms along with his expressive, headstrong, and independent nature characterize him as a person.

Saiful holds a **B.Sc in Electrical and Electronic Engineering** from **Bangladesh University of Engineering and Technology (BUET)**, and an **M.Sc in Computer Science and Engineering** from **BRAC University**. During his undergraduate studies, he actively participated in extracurricular activities, including debating and leadership roles. He acted as the Vice President of the BUET Debating Club and was a member of BUETDC-Nandonik from 2018 to 2020, winning two national championships. He also has a keen interest in logical reasoning and problem-solving, and he has excelled in various academic pursuits, particularly olympiads. He has been a National and Divisional winner in the Bangladesh Mathematical Olympiad and Bangladesh Physics Olympiad. During the lockdown in 2020, he got familiar with the field of Data Science and Machine Learning through online platforms and self-study. This sparked the beginning of his research career in Applied Machine Learning and Deep Learning. Bioinformatics, Digital Signal Processing, Image Processing, and other facets of Computer Vision are also among his research interests. Currently, he is involved in multiple projects focusing on Medical Imaging and Bioinformatics.  
  
In August of 2021, he began teaching at BRAC University. Since joining Brac University, he has taught a variety of courses including Image Processing (CSE428), and coordinated the CSE250 Lab (Circuits & Electronics). He is also a passionate sports enthusiast and a die-hard admirer of both Real Madrid and Bangladesh Cricket. The exciting areas of Bioinformatics and Sports Analytics are where he hopes to use his expertise in the future.

## Dr. Muhammad Nur Yanhaona

- **Source URL**: https://cse.bracu.ac.bd/faculty_profile/188/dr_muhammad_nur_yanhaona

### Profile Details

- **Name**: Dr. Muhammad Nur Yanhaona

### Research Interest

**Policy for Thesis Advising:**

My core interest is in theory, protocol, and algorithmic problems, let it be about parallel computing, graph theory, distributed systems, security, or even information retrieval. I occasionally work on application problems but that happens only when I am deeply interested in a particular problem – not in its application area of computer science in general. I have the conviction that any computer science research should advance computer science some way. A work that solely solves a problem in some other domain using existing computer science tools/techniques is a research in that other domain, which may be of great importance and worthy of publication in the topmost international venue. Regardless, it does not count as computer science research. 

Students intending to work with me should know that I expect them to work very hard and meet me regularly. I only give students problems that I believe to be difficult and know to require extensive literature study. At a time, I can advise only two/three undergraduate research groups and one or two masters students as advising students requires significant time investment on brainstorming, meetings, and literature review on my part.   
  
I believe good research work should be published in good conferences/journals. However, I am not a publication-focused person and do not want to pursue problems that are low hanging fruits regarding potential publications. My expectation from undergraduate thesis groups is that they should learn how to conduct research by working on interesting, unsolved problems. The focus should be on learning – not on publications – at that knowledge level. Master students should solve at least one important problem of international interest and have some good conference/journal publications.  
  
**Fields of Interest:**

1. Graph Theory and Algorithms
2. Network Science Related Problems
3. Protocols and Models of Distributed Systems
4. Distributed AI or Multi-agent Systems
5. Code Reverse Engineering, Code Lifting, and Side-channel Attack Prevention
6. Parallel Programming Language and Algorithms for High-performance Computing

### Biography

Muhammad Nur Yanhaona completed his bachelor's in Computer Science & Engineering from Bangladesh University of Engineering & Technology (BUET) and got his master's and Ph.D. degrees from the University of Virginia, USA. He worked in the software industry for a decade in various roles, including software development, system architecture design, team leading, and design consultancy. He resigned from the software industry as a distinguished software architect and joined BRAC as a faculty member in 2021. His research and development work spans multiple domains, including blockchain technology, application security, databases, wide-area distributed systems, graph theory, and high-performance computing & programming languages.

### Publication

To access my publications, follow the link to my semantic scholar page:  
<https://www.semanticscholar.org/author/Muhammad-N.-Yanhaona/2346479>

## Mr. Moin Mostakim

- **Source URL**: https://cse.bracu.ac.bd/faculty_profile/191/mr_moin_mostakim

### Profile Details

- **Name**: Mr. Moin Mostakim
- **Designation**: Senior Lecturer
- **Email**: mostakim@bracu.ac.bd

### Office Location / Address

- CSE Department
- 4th floor, Room No # 4G02,
- Brac University,
- Kha 224 Bir Uttam Rafiqul Islam Avenue,
- Merul Badda, Dhaka, Bangladesh

### Research Interest

1. **Neural Networks**
2. **NLP**
3. **Distributed Networking**
4. **LLM**
5. **Advance Data Structure**
6. **Algorithms**
7. **Reinforcement Learning**

**Current Interest upon topics :**

1. Develop a large language model capable of generating Bangla Government papers.
2. Summarize the requirements analysis and prepare a suitable semantic requirements analysis.
3. Investigate the use of transformers in generating Docker.
4. Build automated testing tools for inspecting the vulnerabilities of any website.
5. Generate semantic meanings from architectural drawings for the general public.

### Biography

I am **Moin Mostakim**, an individual driven by a deep passion for learning and building knowledge. Throughout my life, I have always been eager to stay up-to-date with cutting-edge technology and immerse myself in the world of research. My journey as a **neural network** architecture specialist and a blissful educator has been a fulfilling one, marked by continuous growth and exploration.

From an early age, I discovered my insatiable curiosity for understanding how things work. This innate curiosity led me to dive into the realm of technology, where I quickly became captivated by the intricate workings of neural networks. The ability of these systems to mimic the human brain and process complex information fascinated me beyond measure.

As I embarked on my educational journey, I dedicated myself to mastering the field of neural network architecture. I pursued a rigorous academic path, obtaining a degree in Computer Science and specialising in artificial intelligence. The more I delved into the subject, the more captivated I became by the immense potential of neural networks to revolutionise various domains, from healthcare to finance and beyond.

Driven by a relentless pursuit of knowledge, I embarked on numerous research projects, seeking to push the boundaries of what was possible in the field of neural networks. I embraced the challenges that arose, from designing innovative architectures to optimising performance and tackling complex problems. Through these research endeavours, I honed my skills and gained invaluable insights into the intricacies of neural network development.

Alongside my research endeavours, I found immense joy in sharing my knowledge with others. I discovered a passion for teaching and mentoring, realising the profound impact I could have on aspiring minds. Guiding students and fellow researchers in their exploration of neural networks brought me great satisfaction and allowed me to contribute to the growth of the field.

As a blissful educator, I foster an environment of curiosity, creativity, and critical thinking. I believe in empowering students to question the status quo, challenge existing paradigms, and push the boundaries of what is known. By instilling a deep understanding of neural network architecture and its applications, I aim to inspire the next generation of innovators and problem solvers.

My journey as Moin Mostakim has been one of continuous learning and growth. I remain passionate about staying at the forefront of technological advancements, embracing the ever-evolving landscape of neural network architecture. Through my research and educational endeavours, I aspire to contribute meaningfully to the field and make a lasting impact on the world.

### Professional Activity

As Moin Mostakim, my professional activities encompass various roles and responsibilities, including being an algorithm teacher, researcher, neural network teacher, and fuzzy system specialist. Throughout my academic career, both in my MSc program and during my undergraduate and graduate studies, I have actively engaged in these roles and contributed to the field in the following ways:

**1. Algorithm Teacher:** I have had the privilege of teaching algorithm courses, sharing my knowledge and expertise with students. Through interactive lectures, assignments, and discussions, I have guided students in understanding the fundamental principles, techniques, and applications of algorithms.

**2. Researcher:** As a researcher, I have dedicated my time to exploring innovative approaches and pushing the boundaries of knowledge in various domains. I have actively participated in research projects, collaborating with fellow researchers, and contributing to the advancement of cutting-edge technologies.

**3. Neural Network Architect:** Teaching neural network courses has been a rewarding experience for me. I have had the opportunity to introduce students to the fascinating world of neural networks, covering topics such as architecture, training algorithms, and applications. Guiding students in their understanding and implementation of neural networks has been immensely fulfilling.

**4. Fuzzy System Specialist:** Within the realm of fuzzy systems, I have specialized in designing and developing intelligent systems that incorporate uncertainty and imprecision. By applying fuzzy logic principles, I have explored the possibilities of building robust and adaptive systems capable of handling complex real-world scenarios.

**5. MSc. Programme**: During my MSc program, I actively participated in coursework, research projects, and seminars relevant to my areas of specialisation. I immersed myself in advanced topics related to algorithms, neural networks, and fuzzy systems, acquiring in-depth knowledge and honing my skills.

6. **Undergraduate and Graduate Thesis Taker:** Throughout my academic journey, I have taken on the responsibility of guiding and supervising undergraduate and graduate students in their thesis projects. This involved providing mentorship, offering research guidance, and ensuring the successful completion of their thesis work.

These professional activities have allowed me to contribute to the field, share my knowledge with students, and actively engage in research and innovation. I have found immense joy and fulfilment in these roles, continually striving to expand my expertise and make a positive impact in the academic community.

## Dr. Md. Khalilur Rahman

- **Source URL**: https://cse.bracu.ac.bd/faculty_profile/193/dr_md_khalilur_rahman

### Profile Details

- **Name**: Dr. Md. Khalilur Rahman

### Research Interest

Robotics, Vision based AI, Multi-Modal AI, Vision based Automation

### Biography

**Dr. Md. Khalilur Rahman** is an Associate Professor of Computer Science and Engineering Department of BRAC University. He was the principal investigator of Nation’s first Nano-satellite program which is named as “BRAC ONNESHA” by BRAC University. He was assigned as the Acting Chairman of CSE department from 1st Sep-14 to 31st Dec-2014. He is the founder of Nano-Satellite Technology Research (NASTER) Lab, BRAC University Robotics lab and the founder advisor of BRAC University Robotics club. The robot named CHONDROBOT, MONGOL-TORI and BRACU-DUBURI was designed and built under his peer supervision. Different versions of these Robots are regularly attending in different international competitions such as NASA Lunabotic Mining Competition USA, University Rover Challenge USA, Singapore Autonomous Underwater Vehicle Competition etc. Dr. Khalil has joined in BRAC University on 2009 immediately after completing his PhD degree from Kyushu Institute of Technology, Japan and promoted as associate professor on 2013. He was a MONBUKAGAKUSHO scholar and his PhD Research topic was Natural Language Processing. But now his research is diverted into Space Science & Engineering, Robotics and Embedded Systems. He was born on 1976 in a river bank city Sirajganj of Bangladesh. After completing his Secondary and Higher Secondary Schooling he took admission in Institute of Science and Technology under National University for graduation and post-graduation degrees. Dr. Khalil became point of contact (POC) of UNISEC-Global (University Space Engineering Consortium) on 2014. In his research and work he could build a very good relationship with government organizations like BTRC, SPARRSO, Atomic Energy Commission, CARGS, ICT Ministry etc. He is also a technical committee member of a Number of International conferences and Journals. He is an IEEE member. S21KT is his amateur radio call sign.

### Professional Activity

**Other Academic engagements:**

* Founder of Robotics Lab of Brac University under which tens of robotics and industrial automation researches are being conducted. Followings are few signature R&D under this Lab
  + UGV and Space Rover Development. This lab became pioneer to develop country’s first space related robot named Chondrobot to compete in Lunabotic Mining Competition arranged by NASA, USA. A Mars Rover names Mongol-Tori is also developed for attending in University Rover Challenge for last 10 years. Currently the research team is designing and developing the space robot.
  + Underwater Robot Development. Robot names Bracu-Duburi became world runner-up in the world in the Robo-Sub Competition.
  + Space Simulation. A simulation team is regularly attending in KIBO Robot Programming Challenge for last few years. Their main challenge is to develop software for space robot like Astrobee. Recently the lab initiated a collaboration to work with MIT Zero Robotics lab and Lincoln Lab.
* Founder of Brac University Satellite Lab. With the collaboration with Kyushu Institute of Technology Bracu own nation’s first nano-satellite named Brac Onnesha that was deployed from ISS on 2016. Brac University join UNISEC Global on 2015.
* Principal Investigator of Autonomous Vehicle project under RSGI research fund of Brac University from Sept 24 to till date where we will be achieving level-4 autonomy
* Co-Principal Investigator of Soft Robotics project under RSGI research fund of Brac University from July 24 to till date where the team is developing a prosthetic claw to help paralyzed patients
* Founding Advisor of Robotics Club of Brac University from 2011 to till date to coordinate and arranging Robotics activities, training, workshops and school visits. More than 150 events and activities are arranged under this club platform.
* Advisor of IEEE RAS chapter of Student Branch from 2020 to till date to coordinate Industry Visit and arranging Robotics Seminars.

## Dr. Farig Yousuf Sadeque

- **Source URL**: https://cse.bracu.ac.bd/faculty_profile/194/dr_farig_yousuf_sadeque

### Profile Details

- **Name**: Dr. Farig Yousuf Sadeque

### Research Interest

Applied Natural Language Processing (NLP), Low-resource Language, NLP for Education, Machine Learning (Applications and Ethics), Artificial Intelligence and Green AI, Computational Social Science, Information Retrieval

### Biography

Dr. Farig Sadeque is an Associate Professor of Computer Science and Engineering at BRAC University. Before joining BRAC University, Dr. Sadeque was an Associate Research Scientist at the Educational Testing Service (ETS), the largest private non-profit educational testing organization in the world. He also worked as a postdoctoral research fellow at Harvard University where he was jointly appointed by the Harvard Medical School and Boston Children’s Hospital, and was a part of the Computational Health Informatics Program (CHIP) Laboratory.

Dr. Sadeque completed his PhD in Information Science with a minor in Computer Science from The University of Arizona under the supervision of Dr. Steven Bethard. He completed his undergrad from Bangladesh University of Engineering in Technology, with a major in Computer Science and Engineering. His research interests include Computational Linguistics and Applied Natural Language Processing, Machine Learning, Computational Social Science, Psycholinguisitcs and Sociolinguistics.

### Publication

https://scholar.google.com/citations?user=ULNaeowAAAAJ&hl=en

### Biography

Hello! I am Rakin Bin Rabbani. I am a Computer Science and Engineering graduate from BRAC University. I also did a minor in Business Administration. I am an enthusiastic learner, student tutor with experience of 2 years and a web developer with more than 1 year of experience, a researcher, and problem solver in nature, and eager to explore new technologies. In my spare time, I play football and travel to keep my body, mind, and soul healthy. Because of my love for teaching, I am currently working as a lecturer at BRAC University, the country's best private university according to the 2018 QS Asian University Rankings. My research interests include IoT, machine learning, and cognitive radio networks. My primary goal is to work in an environment where I can learn, apply and enhance my knowledge by interacting with enthusiastic minds. Moreover, I always look forward to contributing to the betterment of the nation and looking forward to implementing my skills and knowledge to solve real-life problems.

## Dr. Farida Chowdhury

- **Source URL**: https://cse.bracu.ac.bd/faculty_profile/211/dr_farida_chowdhury

### Profile Details

- **Name**: Dr. Farida Chowdhury

### Research Interest

My currents research interests are security usability, privacy usability and HCI (Human Computer Interaction).

### Biography

Dr. Farida Chowdhury is a Professor at the Department of Computer Science and Engineering, BRAC University since July 2023. Previously she was a Professor of Computer Science and Engineering at Shahjalal University of Science and Technology (SUST), Sylhet.

She completed her PhD in Computing Science from the [University of Stirling](https://www.stir.ac.uk), UK. Her PhD thesis title was “Structured Peer-to-Peer Overlays for NATed Churn Intensive Networks”. She completed her MSc. in Network and e-Business Centred Computing which was an Erasmus Mundus Joint Master's Programme between the [University of Reading, UK](https://www.reading.ac.uk); [Aristotle University of Thessaloniki, Greece](https://www.auth.gr/en/) and [Universidad Carlos(III) de Madrid, Spain](https://www.uc3m.es/Home).

She is one of the authors of a book titled Digital Technology (ডিজিটাল প্রযুক্তি) of Class VI of National Curriculum and Textbook Board (NCTB), Bangladesh. She was a visiting research scientist at the [Fraunhofer Institute for Applied Information Technology (Fraunhofer FIT)](https://www.fit.fraunhofer.de/en.html), Germany from May, 2022 to August, 2023. During her SUST tenure, she also worked as the Coordinator of Pipilika, the first Bengali search engine, in between August, 2016 to December, 2019.

Her current research interests include Human Computer Interaction (HCI), Security Usability, Privacy and Networking. She published numerous research articles in reputed international academic journals, conferences and workshops.

### Biography

A B M Muntasir Rahman is currently serving as a Lecturer in the Computer Science and Engineering department under the School of Data and Sciences at BRAC University. He holds a Bachelor of Science degree in Computer Science and Engineering from BRAC University, where he graduated with the highest distinction. During his undergraduate years, Muntasir served as a Student Tutor at the Computer Science and Engineering department of BRAC University, a role he held from June 2021 to December 2022. Additionally, he worked as a Student Mentor, providing guidance and assistance to fresher students in making the most of the resources and opportunities available at BRAC University.

Following his graduation, Muntasir worked as a Lecturer in the department of Information Technology & Management (ITM) at Daffodil International University. His primary areas of research interest encompass Artificial Intelligence, Machine Learning and Image Processing.

### Biography

**Md. Shamim Mia** is currently serving as a Lecturer in the Department of Computer Science and Engineering at BRAC University. Prior to this role, he held positions as a Contractual Lecturer and a Graduate Teaching Assistant in the same department. While serving as a Graduate Teaching Assistant under the supervision of [**Dr. Sadia Hamid Kazi**](https://cse.sds.bracu.ac.bd/faculty_profile/180/dr_sadia_hamid_kazi), he gained valuable teaching experience and deepened his knowledge of computer networks. During his undergraduate studies, he also served as a Student Tutor in the same department for four semesters.

Shamim earned his Bachelor of Science in Computer Science and Engineering from BRAC University in 2023, graduating with the highest distinction. He was also awarded the Vice Chancellor's Gold Medal at the 15th convocation of BRAC University. Before his undergraduate studies, he completed his Higher Secondary Certificate (HSC) from Notre Dame College, Dhaka, in 2018. He consistently excelled in his academic studies, achieving performance-based scholarships from BRAC University and merit-based Government scholarships in all Board Exams.

### Biography

Greetings!

I'm Naveed, a graduate of BRAC University, having wrapped up my studies in fall of 2021. I'm currently wearing two exciting hats: one as a passionate contractual lecturer and the other as a vigilant Software Quality Assurance (SQA) engineer at a well-regarded software firm. I really enjoy learning about cybersecurity and the latest in machine learning.

## Md. Farhadul Islam

- **Source URL**: https://cse.bracu.ac.bd/faculty_profile/218/md_farhadul_islam

### Profile Details

- **Name**: Md. Farhadul Islam
- **Designation**: Research Assistant
- **Email**: farhadul.islam@bracu.ac.bd
- **Websites**: Available on portal

### Biography

[**Md. Farhadul Islam**](https://sites.google.com/g.bracu.ac.bd/mdfarhadulislam) graduated from BRAC University with a BSc in Computer Science. He is currently appointed as a Graduate Research Assistant at the Dept. of CSE, BRAC University under the supervision of [Dr. Jannatun Noor](https://sites.google.com/site/jannatun0abigzero). Mr. Islam is currently involved in research on Resource Efficiency and Reliability in Deep Learning, focusing on the fields of Medical Imaging, Agriculture, Computer Vision, and Natural Language Processing. Currently, he has peer-reviewed publications in journals, conferences, and workshops such as Elsevier Engineering Applications of Artificial Intelligence, Computers in Biology and Medicine, Nature Scientific Reports, WACV, ACMSE, SoutheastCon, S+SSPR, COMPASS, ICMV, etc. His main research interests are Deep Learning, Medical Image Analysis, Computer Vision, and Sustainable Computing. He also actively reviews in various journals and conferences. In addition to research activities, he is a part-time data science instructor at HerWILL where he conducts project-based lectures on data science and machine learning.

## Fahim-Ul-Islam

- **Source URL**: https://cse.bracu.ac.bd/faculty_profile/220/fahimulislam

### Profile Details

- **Name**: Fahim-Ul-Islam

### Biography

**About Me**
------------

Hello! I am Fahim-Ul-Islam, currently working as a **Graduate Research Assistant** in Computer Science and Engineering at Brac University. under the supervision of[**Amitabha Chakrabarty**.](https://www.bracu.ac.bd/about/people/amitabha-chakrabarty-phd) Besides, I am pursuing my **master's** degree at **Brac University**, where I am engaged in my thesis and have nearly completed my coursework.  I have also done my **Bachelor's** in Computer Science and Engineering at **Brac University**. My research interest can be mainly summarized as follows:

**Efficient learning approaches for edge intelligence**

**Robust machine learning for autonomous systems**

**Cutting-edge solutions for Language Understanding**

In the domain of Computer Vision and Graphics, my research is driven by a profound interest in leveraging Artificial Intelligence (AI) to enhance the comprehension and interpretation of visual data. My work aims to advance the state-of-the-art in computer vision algorithms, addressing critical tasks such as object detection, recognition, image generation, and manipulation. By integrating sophisticated deep learning methodologies with computer graphics techniques, I aim to develop systems that can achieve near-human-level accuracy in analyzing and understanding visual content. Additionally, I am deeply interested in Natural Language Processing (NLP) and its multifaceted applications, with particular emphasis on developing Large Language Models (LLMs). I believe that advancements in NLP and LLMs can drive transformative changes across industries by greatly enhancing machines' understanding and processing of human language.

### Biography

Atanu Roy is currently working as a full time Lecturer at Brac University from Fall 2025. Previously we has an Adjunct Lecturer in the same department for 2 years. He was also a System Engineer at Nagad as well as a Technology Intern at the same company for 4 months.

He completed his BSc. in Computer Science and Engineering from Brac University. While he was a student, he worked as a Student Tutor for the CSE department for almost 1.5 years. Alongside that, he also actively participated in various extracurricular activities. His four-year journey at the Robotics Club of Brac University (ROBU) culminated in him holding the position of Director of the Editorial and Publications Department. While being a part of the club, he conducted numerous sessions on the Basics of Robotics. In addition to his involvement with ROBU, he was also associated with the IEEE Computer Society BRACU Student Branch Chapter, where he held the position of Vice Chair in the year 2022.

His educational journey began at St. Joseph Higher Secondary School, where he completed his school and college education.

### Biography

I am a passionate lecturer, researcher, and machine learning engineer with expertise in Machine Learning, Deep Learning, Artificial Intelligence, Federated Learning, and Natural Language Processing. I have extensive experience working on diverse projects that involve developing cutting-edge approaches to solve complex problems in these areas. Currently, I am working as a machine learning engineer, where I apply my expertise to develop and deploy machine learning models for practical applications. Currently, I am also serving as a contractual lecturer at BRAC University.

Throughout my time in the field of Machine Learning, I have successfully contributed to the development of custom models and algorithms, significantly improving the performance of existing systems. I am constantly exploring new applications of these technologies to tackle real-world problems and drive innovation. My research interests encompass not only advancing the field of machine learning but also leveraging interdisciplinary knowledge to address broader challenges.

In addition to my practical experience, I have actively contributed to the research community by submitting and publishing several papers in prestigious conferences and journals related to machine learning and artificial intelligence. I am driven by the desire to collaborate with like-minded individuals who share a passion for research, innovation, and pushing the boundaries of what is possible in these exciting fields.

### Research Interest

My research interest lies in the intersection of computer vision and image processing, particularly focusing on developing efficient algorithms for real-time object recognition, scene understanding, and image enhancement. I am particularly fascinated by the potential of deep learning techniques to improve the accuracy and scalability of these systems in complex and dynamic environments. By exploring novel methods in image segmentation, feature extraction, and image restoration, I aim to contribute to advancements in fields like autonomous vehicles, medical imaging, and surveillance.

### Biography

- **Role / Capacity**: Supervisor, Co-supervisor

- **Student Level**: Undergraduate

- **Supervision Type**: Thesis, Project, Internship

### Biography

Sukarna Sarker is a Lecturer in the Department of Computer Science and Engineering at BRAC University. She joined the department as a full-time faculty member in Summer 2025, after serving as an adjunct lecturer since Fall 2023. She holds a Bachelor of Science degree in Computer Science and Engineering from BRAC University, graduating with the highest distinction. During her undergraduate studies, she was awarded a merit-based scholarship for her academic excellence. In addition, she successfully completed a faculty development training program under the Learning and Teaching Innovation Center (LTIC), further strengthening her pedagogical skills.

Prior to her faculty appointment, Ms. Sarker worked as a student tutor, providing academic support to students from both the School of Data and Sciences (MNS) and the Department of Computer Science and Engineering at BRAC University. Her early engagement in teaching and mentoring roles reflects her commitment to academic development and student success.

She currently serves as the course coordinator for CSE420: Compiler Design, overseeing the course’s delivery and academic integrity across multiple sections.

Ms.  Sarker’s professional interests lie in computer science education, compiler design,machine learning and innovative teaching methodologies. She is dedicated to creating an engaging and inclusive learning environment that empowers students to reach their full potential.

### Research Interest

**AI Methods:** Deep learning, computer vision, multi-modal learning, and conditional content generation.

**Biomedical Applications:** Applying AI to detect and analyse neurological and mental health disorders such as schizophrenia using brain imaging and behavioural data.

**Human-Centered AI:** Building ethical, explainable, and assistive AI systems for trustworthy healthcare applications.

### Biography

Yasmin Nadia graduated from Brac University in 2023 with a Bachelor of Science in Computer Science and Engineering.

She began her professional journey as a Software Engineer Trainee at BJIT Ltd., where she worked with the MERN stack from August 2023 to November 2023.

From October 2023, she served as an Adjunct Lecturer, teaching Data Communication, Discrete Mathematics, Computer Networks Theories, Object-Oriented Programming, and Microprocessor Labs. In October 2025, she joined as a Lecturer, continuing to teach the same subjects.

Her research interests include deep learning, computer vision, multimodal learning, and conditional content generation, with applications in neurological disorder detection and healthcare AI.

## Ipshita Bonhi Upoma

- **Source URL**: https://cse.bracu.ac.bd/faculty_profile/259/ipshita_bonhi_upoma

### Profile Details

- **Name**: Ipshita Bonhi Upoma
- **Designation**: Lecturer
- **Email**: ipshita.upoma@bracu.ac.bd

### Office Location / Address

- CSE Department
- 4th floor, Room No # 4K78,
- Brac University,
- Kha 224 Bir Uttam Rafiqul Islam Avenue,
- Merul Badda, Dhaka, Bangladesh

### Research Interest

1. Machine Learning in Computational Astrophysics

2. Physics Informed Neural Network  
3. Quantum Machine Learning

### Biography

I am an aspiring physicist with an MSc in Particles, Strings and Cosmology from Durham University. My MSc. was funded by the Hatfield Lioness Scholarship which I was awarded from a pool of around 500 applicants in 2020.

After completion of my MSc. I resumed my role as a lecturer at the Computer Science and Engineering Department at Brac University in Bangladesh. I have instructed courses on Artificial Intelligence, Simulation and Modeling and Statistics. Currently, I am also conducting a course on Probabilistic Models. 

Majoring both in Physics and Computer Science in my bachelors (BRAC University), I am also enthusiastic about data science, AI and simulation. Especially the applications of these fields in modern day physics and solving social and practical problems intrigue me. Recently I have supervised thesis projects on Application of Physics Informed Neural Networks in Cosmological Parameter Predictions and, Use of Quantum Aware Algorithms in Image Protection in Generative AI. 

I am passionate about equity in STEAM and want to contribute to building a community of students studying fundamental sciences.  I have initiated and coordinated the participation of my home country, Bangladesh in the European Girls’ Mathematical Olympiad and have been the team leader/deputy leader in 2024, 2022 and 2021. In 2023, I started Bangladesh Girls' Mathematics Foundation aiming to empower girls of Bangladesh in Mathematics and STEM fields.

When I am not working, I seek solace in poetry, stories, music and photography. I try to involve myself in different creative activities. I have been the diversity monitor at the Hatfield College's 175th Anniversary Play, Lions in the Bailey. In the 2022/23 academic session, I was elected to serve as the MCR Chair at Hatfield College, Durham University, where I represented and led the postgraduate student body.

I published a book of poetry at the age of 16 and still continue to write poetry in Bangla.

### Research Interest

### Biography

**Niloy Farhan** is currently serving as a Lecturer at BRAC University in Dhaka, Bangladesh. He is currently taking CSE221: Algorithms and CSE330: Numerical Methods courses, enriching their understanding through theory and lab classes. Before joining BRAC University, Niloy was working as Adjunct Lecturer at Dhaka International University. Niloy's journey in academia began as a Student Tutor at the Department of Computer Science and Engineering at BRAC University from Fall 2022 to Summer 2023.

Niloy Farhan earned his Bachelor of Science in Computer Science from BRAC University, graduating with a remarkable CGPA of 3.94 out of 4.00 in September 2023. Prior to this, he completed his Higher Secondary Certificate from Notre Dame College and his Secondary School Certificate from Motijheel Govt. Boys’ High School, both with outstanding achievements.

Dedicated to academic research, Niloy has made significant contributions in the Natural Language Processing (NLP) field. He conducted “Research on the Latest Trends in Bangla Named Entity Recognition” as part of his Bachelor's Thesis at BRAC University, achieving an exceptional grade of A+. Additionally, he has published a paper titled "Ensemble of Gated Recurrent Unit and Convolutional Neural Network for Sarcasm Detection in Bangla," which was presented at the 2023 IEEE 13th Annual Computing and Communication Workshop and Conference. His current research interests include Applied Natural Language Processing, Machine Learning and Deep Learning.

Mr. Towshik Anam Taj has also worked as a Junior SQA Automation Engineer in Automation Solutionz, a Canada based software firm who provides testing solutions for large scale software and mobile applications.

Mr. Towshik Anam Taj is currently serving as an adjunct lecturer in the same department and has experience on teaching a broader range of courses starting from Introduction to Computer Science, Digital Logic Design to teaching advanced level courses such as Artificial Intelligence, Computer Networks etc. His research interests include Artificial Intelligence, Machine Learning, Cyber Security, Cryptography and other subdomains of the mentioned research areas. He has published research articles in the area of Artificial Intelligence and Cryptography under prestigious conferences and journals and he is currently working on numerous research projects which are exploring various research domains such as Computer Vision, Machine Learning associated Cyber Security etc.

## Redowanul Akbar

- Merul Badda, Dhaka, Bangladesh

### Research Interest

Reinforcement Learning; Trustworthy Large Language Models; Human-Centered Computing; Blockchain and Distributed Systems; Robotics; Embedded Systems; Machine Learning; Cybersecurity.

### Biography

Redowanul Akbar is a Lecturer in the Department of Computer Science and Engineering at BRAC University, Bangladesh. He completed his bachelor’s degree in computer science and engineering in 2023 and joined BRAC University as an adjunct faculty member in Spring 2024. Later, in Summer 2024, he was appointed as a full-time lecturer of BRAC University.

His research interests span human - computer interaction, reinforcement learning, large language models, and robotics, with a particular emphasis on human-centered and hardware-aware intelligent systems. He is especially interested in applying machine learning and reinforcement learning techniques to real-world problems involving decision-making, autonomy, and system optimization across both software and embedded platforms.

Alongside his research activities, Redowanul Akbar is actively involved in teaching and curriculum development. He has taught courses including CSE340: Computer Architecture, CSE360: Computer Interfacing, CSE461: Introduction to Robotics, and embedded systems fundamentals. He is known for integrating theoretical foundations with hands-on experimentation, encouraging students to bridge the gap between abstract concepts and practical implementation.

Beyond academia, he is keen on interdisciplinary innovation that connects technological research with societal needs. His long-term academic goal is to pursue advanced research and contribute to impactful teaching and scholarship in computer science and engineering.

### Professional Activity

* **Lecturer**, Department of Computer Science and Engineering, BRAC University (2024–Present)
  + Teaching undergraduate courses including *Computer Architecture*, *Computer Interfacing*, *Introduction to Robotics*, and *Fundamentals*.
  + Designing course materials, assessments, lab manuals, and project evaluations.
* **Course Lab Coordinator**, CSE360: Computer Interfacing, CSE461: Introduction to Robotics
  + Oversight of course structure, lab design, evaluation rubrics, and mark distribution.
  + Coordination with lab instructors and ensuring academic consistency across sections.
* **Researcher**,conducting ongoing research on reducing hallucinations in large language models using reinforcement learning.
  + Exploring human-centered AI, reliability, and societal implications of intelligent systems.
* **Student Tutor**, BRAC University *(5 semesters)*
  + Assisted in teaching core CSE courses and mentoring undergraduate students.
  + Provided academic support through problem-solving sessions and consultations.

### Research Interest

Human Computer Interaction (HCI), Artificial Intelligence (AI) and Bioinformatics.

### Biography

Umme Jannat Taposhi is a Lecturer in the Department of Computer Science and Engineering at BRAC University, Bangladesh. She earned both her Bachelor’s degree (Fall 2023) and Master’s degree (Summer 2025) in Computer Science and Engineering from BRAC University, graduating with the **Highest Distinction** in both. 

Her research interests lie at the intersection of:

* **Human–Computer Interaction (HCI)**
* **Social Computing and Digital Behavior**
* **Digital Health**
* **Usability and User Experience (UX) Analysis**
* **Human–AI Collaboration and Explainable Systems**

Taposhi’s work focuses on understanding how people interact with intelligent technologies across social, educational, and healthcare contexts. She is particularly interested in designing data-driven yet human-centered systems that improve usability, inclusivity, and trust. Through her teaching and research, she aims to bridge the gap between human behavior and computational intelligence to create more inclusive and impactful digital experiences.

## Md Ishmam Tasin

- **Source URL**: https://cse.bracu.ac.bd/faculty_profile/299/md_ishmam_tasin

### Profile Details

- **Designation**: Research Assistant

- **Websites**: Available on portal

### Office Location / Address

- CSE Department
- 4th floor, Room No # TA/RA Room,
- Brac University,
- Kha 224 Bir Uttam Rafiqul Islam Avenue,
- Merul Badda, Dhaka, Bangladesh
- Ishmam is currently a Graduate Research Assistant in the Department of Computer Science and Engineering at Brac University, where he also completed his undergraduate studies in Computer Science. His research focuses on the intersection of privacy, security, HCI, blockchain, artificial intelligence (AI), cloud computing, and augmented/virtual reality (AR/VR). A core emphasis of his work is understanding the human factors involved in interacting with these technologies, examining how people from diverse backgrounds engage with them, the different kinds of challenges that arise in these interactions, and identifying effective ways to address those challenges. He has worked on projects like [SecureUS ](https://techopoly.github.io/SecureUs/)that combine a social computing style platform with security awareness goals. He has published multiple research papers, including works in [IEEE TrustCom,](https://ieee-aiplus.org/2024/trustcom/) [ACM SAC](https://www.sigapp.org/sac/sac2026/), [IEEE Access](https://ieeeaccess.ieee.org/), ACM [COMPASS](https://compass.acm.org/), and [CHIRA](https://chira.scitevents.org/).

### Research Interest

1. HCI, ICTD
2. Usable privacy & Security
3. Blockchain
4. Distributed Computing
5. IAM (Identity Access Management)
6. DeFi (Decentralized Finance)
7. AI Ethics & Responsible AI
8. Usable AI

### Biography

Ishmam is currently a Graduate Research Assistant in the Department of Computer Science and Engineering at Brac University, where he also completed his undergraduate studies in Computer Science. His research focuses on the intersection of privacy, security, HCI, blockchain, artificial intelligence (AI), cloud computing, and augmented/virtual reality (AR/VR). A core emphasis of his work is understanding the human factors involved in interacting with these technologies, examining how people from diverse backgrounds engage with them, the different kinds of challenges that arise in these interactions, and identifying effective ways to address those challenges. He has worked on projects like [SecureUS](https://techopoly.github.io/SecureUs/) that combine a social computing style platform with security awareness goals. He has published multiple research papers, including works in [IEEE TrustCom,](https://ieee-aiplus.org/2024/trustcom/) [ACM SAC](https://www.sigapp.org/sac/sac2026/), [IEEE Access](https://ieeeaccess.ieee.org/), ACM [COMPASS](https://compass.acm.org/), and [CHIRA](https://chira.scitevents.org/).

Broadly, his research aims to explore these key questions:

* How can existing and emerging technologies (such as AI, blockchain, and cloud computing) be leveraged to solve pressing societal problems, particularly in underserved communities?
* How do social, economic, cultural, and interpersonal factors influence the security and privacy, adoption, acceptance, usage patterns, and evolution of these technologies, and how do they inform technological design decisions?
* In what ways do emerging technologies like AI shape various facets of human life, including societies, cultures, norms, and organizations, and how can we guide human-AI interactions toward positive outcomes for human well-being?

### Research Interest:

Human-computer interaction (**HCI**); information, communication technologies, and development (**ICTD**); interaction techniques for AR/VR and wearable devices; usable **privacy** and **security**; **socio-technical** systems and interdisciplinary technology adoption; **blockchain** and **cloud computing** for secure, scalable interactive systems.

### Research Interest

* **Interpretability in AI, ML, and NLP**
* **Robustness and Security in AI, ML, and NLP**

* **Resource-Efficient AI, ML, and NLP**

### Biography

Ariyan Hossain is a Lecturer in the Department of Computer Science and Engineering at Brac University. He holds a Bachelor of Science in Computer Science and Engineering from Brac University, graduating with the highest distinction.

In his role as a Lecturer, Ariyan teaches core courses such as Data Structures (CSE220), Algorithms (CSE221) and an elective course Natural Language Processing (CSE440), actively engaging with students through both theory and lab sessions.

Ariyan also served as a Graduate Research Assistant in the Department of Computer Science and Engineering at Brac University. Under the supervision ofDr. Muhammad Nur Yanhaona, he worked on classifying news article framing using machine learning and natural language processing techniques.

His research interests include machine learning, deep learning, natural language processing, and AI ethics. His undergraduate thesis, supervised by Dr. Farig Yousuf Sadeque, focused on exploring and mitigating gender bias in word embeddings from transformer-based language models, with the findings submitted for publication. Ariyan continues to contribute to academic research while fostering a passion for teaching and mentorship.

### Publication

**Hossain, A**., Hannan, K. M. A., Haque, R., Musarrat, H., Rafa, N. T., Islam, S. B., Dipu, S. A., Sadeque, F. Y. (2023). *Exploration and Mitigation of Gender Bias in Word Embeddings from Transformer-based Language Models.* Manuscript submitted for publication.

## Yeasin Arafat Pritom

- **Source URL**: https://cse.bracu.ac.bd/faculty_profile/310/yeasin_arafat_pritom

### Profile Details

- **Name**: Yeasin Arafat Pritom
- **Designation**: Lecturer
- **Email**: yeasin.pritom@bracu.ac.bd
- **Contact / Ext**: Yeasin Arafat Pritom is currently a postgraduate student in the Department of Electrical and Electronic Engineering (EEE) at the University of Dhaka (DU), Bangladesh. He is also working as a full-time lecturer in the CSE department at BRAC University, Bangladesh. Starting from July 2022, Yeasin has been serving as the chair of the IEEE EDS Student Branch Chapter at DU. Under his capable leadership, the chapter has become one of the most vibrant and promising IEEE student branch chapters in Bangladesh, promoting excellence in research and learning in the area of electron devices. Yeasin`s research focuses on emerging nanoelectronic and nanophotonic devices for low-power computation and energy-harvesting applications. He has been actively involved in science projects and Olympiads since his early years in junior school. Passing a highly competitive entrance exam, with flying colors, Yeasin got admitted to the EEE department of DU, making his dream of becoming an engineer, a reality. In his junior year, he started working as an undergraduate researcher in the Emerging Nanoscale Devices research group in the department. The research projects that he undertook challenged him to analyze complex information, draw logical conclusions, and apply critical thinking skills to solve problems. He also learned a variety of analytical techniques and tools which helped him to approach problems systematically and derive insights from data. Yeasin`s first research paper was published in 2023 in the reputed Nanoscale Advances journal, where he investigated different light-trapping geometries in nanoscale plasmonic solar cells using Finite-Difference Time-Domain (FDTD) simulations. His senior design project focuses on building robust computational models for sub-3 nm forksheet negative capacitance transistors and portraying their electrical behavior. In addition to his research pursuits, his engagement with IEEE EDS Student Branch Chapters has provided him the perfect platform to develop his management and leadership skills, understand team dynamics and initiate collaboration, and cultivate traits such as composure, decisiveness, and dexterity in implementing action plans. In addition, Yeasin loves hiking and participating in adventurous summit expeditions. During his leisure hours, he enjoys swimming and cycling. Yeasin also has a deep affection for pets and shares their home with a beloved cat and a collection of charming birds. Moreover, his passion for gardening extends to the delightful pastime of cultivating a diverse assortment of fruit and flower trees.
- **Websites**: Available on portal

### Biography

Yeasin Arafat Pritom is currently a postgraduate student in the Department of Electrical and Electronic Engineering (EEE) at the University of Dhaka (DU), Bangladesh. He is also working as a full-time lecturer in the CSE department at BRAC University, Bangladesh. Starting from July 2022, Yeasin has been serving as the chair of the IEEE EDS Student Branch Chapter at DU. Under his capable leadership, the chapter has become one of the most vibrant and promising IEEE student branch chapters in Bangladesh, promoting excellence in research and learning in the area of electron devices. Yeasin`s research focuses on emerging nanoelectronic and nanophotonic devices for low-power computation and energy-harvesting applications. He has been actively involved in science projects and Olympiads since his early years in junior school. Passing a highly competitive entrance exam, with flying colors, Yeasin got admitted to the EEE department of DU, making his dream of becoming an engineer, a reality. In his junior year, he started working as an undergraduate researcher in the Emerging Nanoscale Devices research group in the department. The research projects that he undertook challenged him to analyze complex information, draw logical conclusions, and apply critical thinking skills to solve problems. Yeasin`s first research paper was published in 2023 in the reputed Nanoscale Advances journal, where he investigated different light-trapping geometries in nanoscale plasmonic solar cells using Finite-Difference Time-Domain (FDTD) simulations. - Saadat Rafid Ahmed is a Lecturer in the Department of Computer Science and Engineering at BRAC University, Dhaka. He is actively engaged in multiple research projects spanning Computational Biology and Computational Linguistics. Previously, he has worked as a Data Scientist on the ACE team at Robi Axiata Limited, where he focused on implementing AI pipelines and designing Hierarchical models on Robi's 47M User's transnational data and Generative AI tasks maintaining standard industrial norms data governance, security and sustainability. His research is dedicated towards addressing the low-resource nature of the Bangla language in Computational Linguistics paradigm and elevating its standards on an international scale.

### Research Interest

* Computational Linguistics
* Natural Language Processing (NLP)
* Large Language Model (LLM)
* Low-resource Language (Bangla)
* Machine Learning
* Artificial Intelligence
* Computational Social Science
* Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG)

### Biography

**Rafid Ahnaf** is a Lecturer in the Department of Computer Science and Engineering at BRAC University. He completed his B.Sc. in Computer Science and Engineering from BRAC University and has been involved in teaching at the university since 2022, serving as a Student Tutor, Adjunct Lecturer, and currently as a full-time Lecturer. His teaching experience includes courses such as CSE101/CSE103: Introduction to Computing, CSE260: Digital Logic Design, and CSE461: Introduction to Robotics.

His teaching interests focus on combining theoretical concepts with practical, hands-on learning. He has also been serving as the Lab Coordinator for CSE461: Introduction to Robotics, where he contributes to organizing and improving laboratory activities and practical learning experiences for students.

His research interests primarily include **AI in Healthcare, Trustworthy AI, Image Processing, Machine Learning, and Deep Learning**, with a particular interest in applying AI techniques to healthcare and medical applications.

### Research Interest

My research is primarily on computational materials science and nanoelectronic devices, with a particular focus on emerging memory technologies such as phase-change memory (PCM), resistive RAM (RRAM), and magnetoresistive RAM (MRAM). That said, I welcome the opportunity to supervise  or co-supervise projects outside this immediate scope, provided they offer genuine intellectual challenge for both the student and myself.

### Biography

I’m currently working as a Lecturer at BRAC University in the Department of Computer Science and Engineering. I completed my BSc in Electrical and Electronic Engineering from North South University, Bangladesh, graduating in 2023.

I’m a researcher passionate about semiconductors, first-principles calculations, and emerging memory technologies. I also have a growing interest in Bioinformatics and applied machine learning.

I’m actively seeking research opportunities where I can contribute to innovative projects and continue expanding my expertise.

### Publication

Tasneem Mazhar, et al. (2024). Effects of Cell Aspect Ratio and Applied Pulse Parameters on Resistance Drift in Ge2Sb2Te5 PCM Nanodevices. In 19th IEEE Nanotechnology Materials and Devices Conference (IEEE NMDC 2024).

- **Name**: Dr. Swakkhar Shatabda

### Research Interest

Computational Biology

Bioinformatics

Applied Machine Learning

Optimization and Metaheuristic Algorithms

### Biography

Professor Shatabda earned his Ph.D. from Griffith University in 2014, focusing on AI algorithms for protein structure prediction. He has published widely in reputed journals and conferences, served on editorial boards and review panels, and contributed to the Board of Accreditation for Engineering and Technical Education (BAETE), IEB. His international research includes a stint in BioGeM Institute, Italy. He continues to supervise research projects and mentor students, having overseen over thirty undergraduate projects and more than twenty master’s theses. Additionally, he has authored educational books for children on computing.

From 2014 to 2021, he was the Undergraduate Program Coordinator at United International University (UIU), where he significantly contributed to curriculum development and faculty mentoring. He played a key role in securing UIU’s Computer Science and Engineering department's accreditations in 2015 and 2019. Additionally, he led the Institutional Quality Assurance Cell (IQAC) at UIU from 2022 to 2023 and founded Bangladesh’s first undergraduate Data Science program.

### Publications

Please check [Google Scholar Profile](https://scholar.google.com/citations?user=2DhrWFgAAAAJ&hl=en).

### Professional Activities

* Senior Member, Institute of Electrical and Electronics Engineers (IEEE), also member of the IEEE Signal Processing Society
* Member, The Institution of Engineers, Bangladesh (IEB)
* Director, Board of Accreditation for Engineering and Technical Education (BAETE), IEB
* Member, Quality Assurance Cell, Board of Accreditation for Engineering and Technical Education (BAETE), IEB
* Academic Editor, PLOS One
* Machine Learning Consultant, Nodes Digital Limited

### Funded Projects

* **Bangla Computing** Development of a Bengali document layout analysis system in the newspaper domain. Amount: BDT 495k, IAR-UIU Research Grants.
* **Genome Assembly** Probabilistic methods for filling gaps in genome assemblies. Amount: BDT 410k, UIU Research Grants.
* **Bangla NLP** Development of Deep Learning based Bangla Spell and Grammar Checker. Amount: BDT 499K, IAR-UIU Research Grants
* **Image Analysis** Prediction of Alzheimer’s Disease Using Deep Learning Techniques. Amount: BDT 498K, IAR-UIU Research Grants.
* **Image Segmentation** Semantic Biomedical Image Segmentation Using Attention Mechanism Driven Modified U-Net Based on Deep CNN. Amount: BDT 499K, IAR-UIU Research Grants.
* **Pharmaceutical Chemistry** Screening flavonoids for necroptosis inhibitors using machine learning and molecular dynamics simulations. Amount: BDT 499K, IAR-UIU -OR-NSU Research Grants.

## Md. Asif Haider

- **Source URL**: https://cse.bracu.ac.bd/faculty_profile/342/md_asif_haider

- **Designation**: Lecturer

- Merul Badda, Dhaka, Bangladesh

### Research Interest

* Software Engineering and Security
* Large Language Models
* Human Factors in Computing
* Natural Language Processing
* AI for Healthcare and Social Good

### Biography

**Md. Asif Haider** served as a Lecturer in the Department of Computer Science and Engineering at BRAC University from 2024-25. He earned his BSc. Engg. degree in Computer Science and Engineering from Bangladesh University of Engineering and Technology (BUET) in 2024. During his undergraduate studies, Asif completed two short, hybrid internships at OpenRefactory Bangladesh Limited and North West Power Generation Company Limited, where he focused on Secure Software Engineering and Machine Learning tasks, respectively. Additionally, he had actively mentored high school students for the past few years.

His research interests span Software Engineering, Large Language Models, and Human Factors in Computing and AI. He is also passionate about Machine Learning and Natural Language Processing techniques applied to the Healthcare domain, as well as contemporary topics in computational social science.

Asif is deeply thankful for the mentors and resources he has been blessed with throughout his journey. Driven by a strong enthusiasm for research and development, he is committed to contributing to the academic community with what students need most: quality education, right guidance, and motivating support. He is also an advocate of academic and cultural leadership activities on campus and beyond. When he is not busy with academic/research work, he is likely to be found exploring music, literature, and theaters.

Starting in fall 2025, Asif continues to work as a Graduate Teaching Assistant at the University of California, Irvine while on study leave to pursue his PhD.

## Md. Muhtasim Mahmud

- **Source URL**: https://cse.bracu.ac.bd/faculty_profile/353/md_muhtasim_mahmud

- **Name**: Md. Muhtasim Mahmud

### Research Interest

Machine Learning, Deep Learning, Computer Vision, Imagine processing, 3D Image processing

### Biography

**Md. Muhtasim Mahmud** is currently serving as a **Lecturer** in the Department of Computer Science and Engineering at **BRAC University**. He joined the department as an **Adjunct Lecturer** in October 2024 and was appointed as a full-time Lecturer in January 2026. Prior to his academic appointment, he worked as a **Student Tutor** in the Department of Computer Science and Engineering from 2022 to 2024, where he gained valuable teaching and mentoring experience.

He received his **Bachelor of Science in Computer Science and Engineering** from BRAC University in 2024, graduating with the **Highest Distinction**. Throughout his undergraduate studies, he was awarded a **Merit-Based Scholarship** in recognition of his outstanding academic performance. He is currently pursuing his **Master of Science in Computer Science and Engineering** at BRAC University, where he has completed his coursework and is conducting research for his master's thesis.

In recognition of his academic excellence, Muhtasim received the **Bangladesh Police Academic Excellence Award** in **2017**, **2019**, and **2025** for his exceptional performance in the SSC, HSC, and undergraduate examinations, respectively.

His research interests include **Machine Learning**, **Deep Learning**, **Artificial Intelligence**, **Computer Vision**, **Image and 3D Processing**, and **Explainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI)**. His current research focuses on developing explainable AI techniques to improve the transparency, interpretability, and reliability of intelligent systems.

### Research and Study Interests

I have completed my B.Sc. in EEE from BUET in 2024. My major was in Electrical Energy and Power Systems. However, I am interested in the field of ML & AI as well. I look forward to engaging in research activities and to learn more in these areas, particularly in reinforcement learning and deep learning. Other sectors of my interest include computer vision, embedded systems, IoT etc.

In my early days, I grew a passion for mathematics and computer science. I have had the opportunity to participate and win prizes in several olympiads related to these. I always appreciate critical thinking, and find a genuine interest in problem solving.

## Farhan Faruk

### Research Interest

Machine learning, image segmentation, applications in healthcare.

### Biography

**Farhan Faruk** is currently serving as a Lecturer in the Department of Computer Science and Engineering at BRAC University. Previously, he worked as an Adjunct Lecturer in the same department from October 2024 to December 2025. Prior to this role, he gained teaching experience as a Student Tutor in both the CSE and MNS departments. He also worked in the plagiarism team for the Artificial Intelligence course to maintain academic integrity.

Farhan earned his Bachelor of Science in Computer Science and Engineering from BRAC University in 2024, graduating with the highest distinction. He was featured on the Vice Chancellor’s Honor List for 10 semesters and received a performance-based scholarship. He also received the Best Thesis Award - Spring 2024 from the Department of Computer Science and Engineering for outstanding undergraduate research.

His research interests include machine learning, image segmentation, and their practical applications in healthcare and education.

## K. M. Shadman Wadith

- **Source URL**: https://cse.bracu.ac.bd/faculty_profile/377/k_m_shadman_wadith

### Profile Details

- **Name**: K. M. Shadman Wadith
- **Designation**: Lecturer
- **Email**: shadman.wadith@bracu.ac.bd
- **Contact / Ext**: His research focus is Reinforcement Learning, particularly Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL). He has dedicated his career to exploring scalable, safe, and adaptive solutions for autonomous systems. His research includes privacy-preserving, decentralized MARL algorithms, and his master's research investigates hybrid MARL approaches that bridge offline and online paradigms for adaptive decision-making in dynamic environments. Alongside his core research, his interests extend to trustworthy and robust AI and NLP, with specific challenges in creating a Bangla language model.
- **Websites**: Available on portal

### Office Location / Address

- CSE Department
- 4th floor, Room No # 4M132,
- Brac University,
- Kha 224 Bir Uttam Rafiqul Islam Avenue,
- Merul Badda, Dhaka, Bangladesh
- K.M. Shadman Wadith is a lecturer in the Department of Computer Science and Engineering at BRAC University. Before this, he was a lecturer at the Green University of Bangladesh. He completed his B.Sc. from the Department of CSE, University of Dhaka.

### Research Interest

* Reinforcement Learning
* Multi Modal System
* Multi Agent System
* Natural Language Processing(NLP)
* Machine Learning
* Artificial Intelligence

### Biography

**K.M. Shadman Wadith** is a lecturer in the Department of Computer Science and Engineering at BRAC University. Before this, he was a lecturer at the Green University of Bangladesh. He completed his B.Sc. from the Department of CSE, University of Dhaka.   
His research focus is Reinforcement Learning, particularly Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL). He has dedicated his career to exploring scalable, safe, and adaptive solutions for autonomous systems. His research includes privacy-preserving, decentralized MARL algorithms, and his master's research investigates hybrid MARL approaches that bridge offline and online paradigms for adaptive decision-making in dynamic environments. Alongside his core research, his interests extend to trustworthy and robust AI and NLP, with specific challenges in creating a Bangla language model.

My research interest includes Plasmonics & Opto-electronics.

### Biography

Mohammad Tahsin Alam is currently serving as a full-time lecturer in the Department of Computer Science and Engineering at BRAC University, a role he assumed in July 2024.

He completed his B.Sc. degree from the Department of **Electrical and Electronic Engineering (EEE), BUET** in 2024, specializing in Electronics and Photonics. Tahsin’s research interests center on advanced fields such as Plasmonics and Opto-electronics, and he has conducted work in emerging areas like Perovskite solar cells and 2D materials. As an educator, Tahsin strives to create an engaging and interactive atmosphere for his students in the classroom.

In his free time, he enjoys watching anime and reading books.

## Sazzadul Islam

- **Source URL**: https://cse.bracu.ac.bd/faculty_profile/396/sazzadul_islam

### Profile Details

- **Name**: Sazzadul Islam
- **Designation**: Lecturer
- **Email**: sazzadul.islam@bracu.ac.bd

### Office Location / Address

- CSE Department
- 4th floor, Room No # 4N165,
- Brac University,
- Kha 224 Bir Uttam Rafiqul Islam Avenue,
- Merul Badda, Dhaka, Bangladesh

### Research Interest

2d Materials, IOT, Device Physics

### Biography

Sazzadul Islam is currently serving as a Lecturer in the Department of Computer Science and Engineering at BRAC University. He has an exceptional academic background and numerous achievements in his field. His passion for Electrical and Electronic Engineering led him to pursue a Bachelor of Science degree in Electrical and Electronic Engineering at **Bangladesh University of Engineering and Technology (BUET)**, where he achieved an impressive **CGPA of 3.9515/4.00**, reflecting his dedication to academic excellence.   
Sazzadul Islam's research experience is extensive, with a notable achievement being his undergraduate thesis on **Quantum Transport** and **Device Physics**, completed under the guidance of **Dr. Mahbub Alam**.

In addition to his academic accomplishments, Sazzad has received several prestigious awards and honors. He consistently earned the **Dean's List Award at BUET** for his outstanding academic performance and has been the recipient of multiple scholarships, including the **Talentpool Scholarship** at the secondary level and higher secondary level. Sazzad was also the winner of the **BUET EEE Olympiad** in 2023.  
Sazzadul Islam's achievements extend beyond academics, as he has actively engaged in extracurricular activities and held various leadership roles. He served as the Membership Development Coordinator of the **BUET Robotics Society** from 2022 to 2023, and subsequently took on the role of Deputy Head of Event Management from 2023 to 2024. During this time, he played a key role in organizing and managing events for the society

## Pratyasha Saha

- **Source URL**: https://cse.bracu.ac.bd/faculty_profile/400/pratyasha_saha

### Profile Details

- **Name**: Pratyasha Saha
- **Designation**: Research Assistant
- **Email**: pratyasha.saha@bracu.ac.bd
- **Websites**: Available on portal

### Biography

Pratyasha is a Graduate Research Assistant at the Department of CSE, BRAC University. She also works as a remote Graduate Research Assistant in the [Third Space Research Group](http://thirdspace.toronto.edu/) at the Department of Computer Science, University of Toronto. Prior to this, she worked as a Research Assistant in the [PROTIC II project](https://www.monash.edu/it/empowered-communities/proticII) at the Department of Human Centered Computing, Monash University, Australia. She holds a bachelor’s degree in Physics from the University of Dhaka.

For her research, she is broadly interested in the intersection of technology and society. She uses mixed-methods to study privacy, trust, ethics, bias, and social justice within socio-technical systems.

Over the past six years, she has conducted extensive ethnographic research with diverse groups, including online sexual harassment survivors, juvenile delinquents, sex workers, slum-dwellers, and LGBTQ+, Hijra, Bede, and Harijan communities across the Global South. Her work has been widely recognized and published in top HCI venues such as CHI and CSCW. She has published over 20 research papers, including ten in CHI and CSCW, and others in [ICTD](https://ictd.org/), ACM [DIS](https://dis.acm.org/), [COMPASS](https://compass.acm.org/), [JCSS](https://dl.acm.org/journal/acmjcss), and [CUI](https://cui.acm.org/). Her research has been rewarded with a CHI Best Paper Award in 2024.

I also gained experience in multispectral and hyperspectral data collection in the lab and the field, as well as processing them.

## Niaz Ashraf Khan

- **Source URL**: https://cse.bracu.ac.bd/faculty_profile/409/niaz_ashraf_khan

### Profile Details

- **Name**: Niaz Ashraf Khan
- **Designation**: Senior Lecturer
- **Email**: - Niaz Ashraf Khan is a dedicated academic professional with over seven years of experience in Computer Science and Engineering. He is currently a Senior Lecturer in the Department of Computer Science and Engineering at BRAC University, Dhaka, Bangladesh. Niaz holds a Master’s degree in Computer Science and Engineering from North South University, Dhaka, Bangladesh, where he also completed his Bachelor’s degree. His research interests lie at the intersection of Sound Signal Processing, Natural Language Processing (NLP), Machine Learning, and Deep Learning. He can be contacted via email at niaz.ashraf@bracu.ac.bd.
- **Websites**: Available on portal

### Office Location / Address

- CSE Department
- 4th floor, Room No # 4M125,
- Brac University,
- Kha 224 Bir Uttam Rafiqul Islam Avenue,
- Merul Badda, Dhaka, Bangladesh

### Research Interest

I work at the intersection of Explainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI) and model optimisation, specially pruning and interpretability in machine learning models for healthcare. My research seeks to create AI systems that are accurate, interpretable, and resource-efficient, making them more viable for real-world clinical use.

### Biography

Niaz Ashraf Khan is a dedicated academic professional with over seven years of experience in Computer Science and Engineering. He is currently a Senior Lecturer in the Department of Computer Science and Engineering at BRAC University, Dhaka, Bangladesh. Niaz holds a Master’s degree in Computer Science and Engineering from North South University, Dhaka, Bangladesh, where he also completed his Bachelor’s degree. His research interests lie at the intersection of Sound Signal Processing, Natural Language Processing (NLP), Machine Learning, and Deep Learning. He can be contacted via email at niaz.ashraf@bracu.ac.bd.

### Biography

Sakib Anwar Rieyan joined BRAC University as a Lecturer in the Department of Computer Science and Engineering in February 2025. He holds a BSc in Computer Science and Engineering from BRAC University, graduating with Highest Distinction . His research includes a publication in Information Fusion (Impact Factor: 18.6) on homomorphic encryption and federated learning, and he supervised a student paper on interpretable deep learning, accepted at the 27th International Conference on Computer and Information Technology (2024). With prior experience as a Lecturer at Northern University Bangladesh (2023-2024), Sakib has taught courses such as Artificial Intelligence, Algorithms, Discrete Mathematics, and Numerical Methods.

## Shihab Muhtasim

- **Source URL**: https://cse.bracu.ac.bd/faculty_profile/419/shihab_muhtasim

### Profile Details

- **Name**: Shihab Muhtasim

### Biography

Shihab Muhtasim is an adjunct lecturer in the Department of Computer Science and Engineering at Brac University. He teaches several fundamental and advanced courses, including Object-Oriented Programming, Microprocessors, Artificial Intelligence, Data Structures, and Operating Systems. His teaching approach emphasizes clarity, real-world applications, and hands-on learning to help students build strong problem-solving skills.

Academically, he has demonstrated excellence throughout his career. He graduated as the valedictorian and Chancellor's Gold Medalist from Brac University with a perfect CGPA of 4.0. His passion for computer science extends beyond academics, as he actively engages in research and mentoring.

His research interests lie in network science, natural language processing (NLP), machine learning (ML), computer vision, and image processing. His undergraduate thesis explored the intersection of network science and machine learning, achieving a score of 99.56 out of 100. The work focused on analyzing complex social networks in order to predict the popularity of social trends that spread through hashtags. He is currently in the process of publishing this research, aiming to contribute to the broader scientific community.

Beyond research, he has experience in software development, competitive programming, and academic mentoring. His dedication to teaching and research reflects his commitment to fostering a strong learning environment. He continues to explore new methodologies in AI and computing, aiming to inspire the next generation of engineers and researchers.

### Awards

1. Chancellor's Gold Medalist  at BRAC University
2. Valedictorian of the 16th Convocation at BRAC University
3. 100% Merit Scholarship at BRAC University
4. VC's List for all semesters at BRAC University
